\left\lVert Q^{(a)}-Q^{(b)}\right\rVert_{Y}\leq\epsilon^{-\beta}\,c(\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(b)})\sum_{\ell=1}^{3}\left\lVert\kappa_{\ell}^{(a)}-\kappa_{\ell}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{Y}

where the norms
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{Y}
are given by (
199
) and
\beta=0
for the
L^{\infty}
bound.
Here and throughout, we note that if the two curves
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
are sufficiently close in the sense of Lemma
1.1
, then the bounds of the form (
213
) imply that

(214)

\left\lVert Q^{(a)}-Q^{(b)}\right\rVert_{Y}\leq\epsilon^{-\beta}\,c(\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(b)})\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\beta}(\mathbb{T})}\,.

Given all of the above expansions, we now state a series of lemmas that will be used to estimate the actual boundary integral expressions appearing in the decomposition (
66
) and to obtain Lemmas
1.10
,
1.11
, and
1.12
. Each of the following (Lemmas
4.1
-
4.4
) comes directly from
[
59
, section 3]
and will thus be stated here without proof.

Lemma 4.1

(Relating
\bm{R}
and
\overline{\bm{R}}
)
.

Given
\overline{\bm{R}}
,
\bm{R}
as in (
196
), (
205
), respectively, the following bounds hold for
\epsilon
sufficiently small:

(215)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert-|\overline{\bm{R}}|\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa_{*}}{2}\widehat{s}^{2}+c(\kappa_{*})\,\epsilon\left\lvert\widehat{s}\right\rvert

(216)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\geq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})|\overline{\bm{R}}|\,.

In addition to Lemma
4.1
, we have the following series of integral estimates.

Lemma 4.2

(Integral bounds for
\bm{R}
and
\overline{\bm{R}}
)
.

Given
\bm{R}
,
\overline{\bm{R}}
as in (
205
), (
196
), respectively, and given
0\leq\alpha<1
, let
k
be an integer satisfying
k-\alpha<2
. We have

(217)

\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{k-\alpha}}\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\overline{\bm{R}}\right\rvert^{k-\alpha}}\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\;\leq\begin{cases}c\,\epsilon^{2-k+\alpha}\,,&1<k-\alpha<2\\
c\,\epsilon\,,&k-\alpha\leq 1\,.\end{cases}

Lemma 4.3

(Bound for integrands with odd powers)
.

Let
\overline{\bm{R}}
,
\bm{R}
be as in (
196
), (
205
) and let
\ell,k,n,m
be nonnegative integers with
\ell+k+2=n+m
and
\ell+k
odd. Given
g,\varphi\in C^{0,\alpha}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})
, we have

(218)

\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\widehat{s}^{\ell}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}g(\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{m}|\bm{R}|^{n}}\varphi(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{\alpha}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

Here
{\rm p.v.}
is the Cauchy principal value, given along
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
by

\displaystyle{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}h(\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}=\lim_{\delta\to 0^{+}}\bigg(\int_{-1/2}^{-\delta}+\int_{\delta}^{1/2}\bigg)\bigg(\int_{-\pi}^{-2\pi\delta}+\int_{2\pi\delta}^{\pi}\bigg)h(\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

Finally, we state the following bound for the
\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}
seminorm of integral operators along
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
.

Lemma 4.4

(Estimating
\left\lvert\cdot\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}
seminorms)
.

Let
\ell,k,m,n
be nonnegative integers satisfying either

(1)

\ell+k=m+n-1
or

(2)

\ell+k=m+n-2
and
\ell+k
is odd.

Given
g(s,\theta,s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\in C^{0,\alpha^{+}}(\Gamma_{\epsilon}\times\Gamma_{\epsilon})
for any
0<\alpha<\alpha^{+}<1
, for
\overline{\bm{R}}
and
\bm{R}
as in (
196
), (
205
), let
K_{\ell kmn}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})
denote

(219)

K_{\ell kmn}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta}):=\frac{\widehat{s}^{\ell}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}g(s,\theta,s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})}{\left\lvert\overline{\bm{R}}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\right\rvert^{m}\left\lvert\bm{R}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\right\rvert^{n}}\,.

Suppose
\varphi(s,\theta)\in C^{0,\alpha}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})
. Then for any
(s_{0},\theta_{0})\in[-1/2,1/2]\times[-\pi,\pi]
with
s_{0}^{2}+\theta_{0}^{2}\neq 0
we have

(220)

\displaystyle\bigg|{\rm p.v.}\int_{-s_{0}-1/2}^{-s_{0}+1/2}\int_{-\theta_{0}-\pi}^{-\theta_{0}+\pi}K_{\ell kmn}(s_{0}+s,\theta_{0}+\theta,s_{0}+\widehat{s},\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta})\varphi(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle-{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}K_{\ell kmn}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\varphi(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{1}}\left\lVert\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}&\text{in case (1)}\\
c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\big(\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha^{+}}_{1}}+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}\big)\left\lVert\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}&\text{in case (2)}\,,\end{cases}

where
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{1}}
and
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}
are as in (
199
).

4.2.
Single layer remainder terms

Given the tools of section
4.1
, we may proceed to the proof of Lemma
1.10
regarding the mapping properties of the single layer remainder
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S}}
, defined in (
65
). We restate the lemma here for convenience.

Lemma 4.5

(Single layer remainder)
.

Let
0<\alpha<\beta
and consider a filament
\Sigma_{\epsilon}
with centerline
\bm{X}(s)\in C^{2,\beta}(\mathbb{T})
. Let the single layer remainder
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S}}
be as defined in (
65
), and let the map
\Phi
be as in (
27
). Given
\bm{\varphi}\in C^{0,\alpha}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})
with
\int_{\mathbb{T}}\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi}(s,\theta)\,ds=0
,
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S}}
may be decomposed as

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S}}[\bm{\varphi}]=\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},\epsilon}[\bm{\varphi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},+}[\bm{\varphi}]

where

(221)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},\epsilon}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,\quad\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},\epsilon}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1,\alpha}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},+}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,\quad\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},+}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1,\beta}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

Furthermore, given two nearby filaments with centerlines
\bm{X}^{(a)}(s)
,
\bm{X}^{(b)}(s)
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, let
\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}=\bm{\varphi}-\int_{\mathbb{T}}(\Phi^{(a)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}(s,\theta)\,ds
and
\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}=\bm{\varphi}-\int_{\mathbb{T}}(\Phi^{(b)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}(s,\theta)\,ds
. The difference between the corresponding single layer remainders
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S}}^{(a)}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S}}^{(b)}
may be decomposed as above, and the components satisfy

(222)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},\epsilon}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},\epsilon}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},\epsilon}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},\epsilon}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{1,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},+}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},+}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},+}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},+}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{1,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\beta}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

Proof of Lemma
1.10
.

Recall the definition of
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S}}
from (
65
) and note that by assumption
\bm{\varphi}(s,\theta)=\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi}(s,\theta)
since we consider
\int_{\mathbb{T}}\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi}(s,\theta)\,ds=0
. Rewriting each integrand in terms of the surface parameters
s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta}
, we may decompose
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S}}
as follows:

(223)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S}}[\bm{\varphi}(s,\theta)]

\displaystyle=\Phi^{-1}\mathcal{S}[\bm{\varphi}(s,\theta)]-\overline{\mathcal{S}}[\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi}(s,\theta)]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}[\bm{\varphi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1}[\bm{\varphi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2}[\bm{\varphi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}[\bm{\varphi}]\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=-\bigg(\int_{-\infty}^{-1/2}+\int_{1/2}^{\infty}\bigg)\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\overline{\mathcal{G}}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})\,\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi}\bigg(\Phi^{-1}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}\bm{\varphi}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{s}\,d\widehat{\theta}

\displaystyle-\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|}(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi})(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{s}\,d\widehat{\theta}\bigg)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi}\bigg(\Phi^{-1}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\bm{R}\otimes\bm{R}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}\bm{\varphi}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{s}\,d\widehat{\theta}

\displaystyle-\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\overline{\bm{R}}\otimes\overline{\bm{R}}}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi})(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{s}\,d\widehat{\theta}\bigg)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=-\Phi^{-1}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\mathcal{G}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})\bm{\varphi}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

We begin with bounds for the smooth remainder
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}
away from the singularity at
(\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})=(0,0)
. Since
\int_{\mathbb{T}}\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi}(s,\theta)\,ds=0
, we may write
\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi}(s,\theta)=\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{\varphi}}(s,\theta)
for some
\overline{\bm{\varphi}}
with
\overline{\bm{\varphi}}(0,\theta)=0
.
Using
\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{\varphi}}(s,\theta)
in the expression for
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}
, we may then integrate by parts in
\widehat{s}
to obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=-\bigg(\int_{-\infty}^{-1/2}+\int_{1/2}^{\infty}\bigg)\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\big(\partial_{\widehat{s}}\,\overline{\mathcal{G}}\big)\;\overline{\bm{\varphi}}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\overline{\mathcal{G}}\;\overline{\bm{\varphi}}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg|_{\widehat{s}=-1/2}^{\widehat{s}=1/2}\,.

Using the form (
37
) of the Stokeslet kernel
\mathcal{G}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}\right\rvert\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\overline{\bm{\varphi}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{1/2}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\widehat{s}^{2}}\,d\widehat{s}+c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\overline{\bm{\varphi}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\overline{\bm{\varphi}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}=c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\int_{0}^{s}(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi})\,ds^{\prime}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

Furthermore, we may estimate

\displaystyle\left\lVert\partial_{s}^{2}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{1/2}^{\infty}\bigg(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\widehat{s}\right\rvert^{3}}+\frac{\epsilon}{\left\lvert\widehat{s}\right\rvert^{4}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\left\lvert\widehat{s}\right\rvert^{5}}\bigg)\,d\widehat{s}\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

Finally, given two nearby curves
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, the corresponding remainder pieces
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}^{(a)}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}^{(b)}
are nearly identical except for slight differences in the
s
-means
\int_{\mathbb{T}}(\Phi^{(a)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}\,ds
and
\int_{\mathbb{T}}(\Phi^{(b)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}\,ds
. We may write

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\qquad=\left\lvert\bigg(\int_{-\infty}^{-1/2}+\int_{1/2}^{\infty}\bigg)\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\overline{\mathcal{G}}\,((\Phi^{(a)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}-(\Phi^{(b)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}})\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left\lvert\bigg(\int_{-\infty}^{-1/2}+\int_{1/2}^{\infty}\bigg)\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\big(\partial_{\widehat{s}}\,\overline{\mathcal{G}}\big)\;(\overline{\bm{\varphi}}^{(a)}-\overline{\bm{\varphi}}^{(b)})\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left\lvert\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\overline{\mathcal{G}}\;(\overline{\bm{\varphi}}^{(a)}-\overline{\bm{\varphi}}^{(b)})\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg|_{\widehat{s}=-1/2}^{\widehat{s}=1/2}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\qquad\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\overline{\bm{\varphi}}^{(a)}-\overline{\bm{\varphi}}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\int_{0}^{s}\big((\Phi^{(a)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}-(\Phi^{(b)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}\big)\,ds^{\prime}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert(\Phi^{(a)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}-(\Phi^{(b)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*}^{(a)},\kappa_{*}^{(b)})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,

by the bound (
81
). By a similar calculation, we additionally obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert\partial_{s}^{2}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\partial_{s}^{2}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert(\Phi^{(a)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}-(\Phi^{(b)})^{-1}\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*}^{(a)},\kappa_{*}^{(b)})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2}}\,\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

In summary, the remainder term
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}
satisfies the estimates

(224)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rVert_{C^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rVert_{C^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*}^{(a)},\kappa_{*}^{(b)})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2}}\,\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

We now turn to the remainders
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2}
, which both contain some components which are small in
\epsilon
and some components which are more regular. To separate these components, we define an auxiliary function

(225)

\bm{R}_{\rm t}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})=\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)+\epsilon\big(\bm{e}_{r}(s,\theta)-\bm{e}_{r}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\big)\,,

as in
[
59
, Section 3.2]
, and note that

(226)

\displaystyle|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{2}

\displaystyle=\textstyle|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{2}+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{S,1}+\epsilon^{2}\widehat{s}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q_{S,2}

\displaystyle|\bm{R}|^{2}

\displaystyle=\textstyle|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{2}+\widehat{s}^{3}Q_{S,3}+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q_{S,4}\,,

where the terms
Q_{S,j}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})
are given by

\displaystyle Q_{S,1}

\displaystyle=\textstyle-2\widehat{\kappa}(s,\theta)+\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}(s,\theta)^{2}+\epsilon\kappa_{3}^{2}-4\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)\cdot\bm{Q}_{\theta}(s,\widehat{s},\theta-\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\textstyle 2\widehat{s}Q_{0,1}(s,\widehat{s},\theta)+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{0,4}(s,\widehat{s},\theta)

\displaystyle Q_{S,2}

\displaystyle=\textstyle 4\kappa_{3}\cos(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})+4\widehat{s}Q_{0,3}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})-4\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{Q}_{r}(s,\widehat{s},\theta)+4\widehat{s}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\left\lvert\bm{Q}_{\theta}\right\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle Q_{S,3}

\displaystyle=\textstyle 2\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{Q}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\bm{Q}_{\rm t}(s,\widehat{s})\cdot\bm{Q}_{r}(s,\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}\left\lvert\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\right\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle Q_{S,4}

\displaystyle=\textstyle-4\bm{Q}_{\rm t}(s,\widehat{s})\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta}(s,\theta-\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})

for
Q_{0,j}
as in (
202
).
In addition, each
Q_{S,j}
satisfies an estimate of the form (
213
).
We emphasize that the difference between
\bm{R}_{\rm t}
and
\bm{R}
is higher order in
\widehat{s}
and the difference between
\bm{R}_{\rm t}
and
\overline{\bm{R}}
is higher order in
\epsilon
.
Furthermore,
\bm{R}_{\rm t}
may be shown to satisfy the same estimates as
\bm{R}
in each of Lemmas
4.1
,
4.2
,
4.3
, and
4.4
(see
[
59
]
).

For integers
k\geq 1
, we additionally note the following expansions:

(227)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{k}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{k}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{s}^{3}Q_{S,3}+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q_{S,4}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|(\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert+|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|)}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k-1}\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{\ell}|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{k-1-\ell}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{k}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{k}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{S,1}+\epsilon^{2}\widehat{s}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q_{S,2}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}||\overline{\bm{R}}|(|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|+|\overline{\bm{R}}|)}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k-1}\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{\ell}|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{k-1-\ell}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(a)}\big|^{k}}-\frac{1}{\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(b)}\big|^{k}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}(Q_{S,1}^{(a)}-Q_{S,1}^{(b)})+\epsilon^{2}\widehat{s}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(Q_{S,2}^{(a)}-Q_{S,2}^{(b)})}{\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(a)}\big|\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(b)}\big|\big(\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(a)}\big|+\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(b)}\big|\big)}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k-1}\frac{1}{\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(a)}\big|^{\ell}\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(b)}\big|^{k-1-\ell}}\,.

Here, as usual, the superscripts
(a)
and
(b)
are used to denote quantities corresponding to two different but nearby filament centerlines
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
, respectively, satisfying Lemma
1.1
.

Using
\bm{R}_{\rm t}
, we further subdivide
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi}\big(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}[\bm{\varphi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}[\bm{\varphi}]\big)\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=\Phi^{-1}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\bigg(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|}\bigg)\bm{\varphi}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|}\bigg)\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{\varphi}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

Here we take the subscript
j\in\{{\rm t},r,\theta\}
and use
\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}
to denote the corresponding basis vector with respect to the straight frame about
\mathcal{C}_{\epsilon}
. Note that in this notation,
\overline{\bm{e}}_{\rm t}=\bm{e}_{z}
.
We additionally subdivide
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi}\big(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}[\bm{\varphi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}[\bm{\varphi}]\big)\,,

\displaystyle\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\bigg(\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{R})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\bigg(\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi}))}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

Using the above expressions along with the forms of
\overline{\bm{R}}
(
196
),
\bm{R}
(
205
),
\bm{R}_{\rm t}
(
225
), and the differences (
209
), (
210
), and (
227
), by Lemma
4.2
we have

(228)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*})\,\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\leq c(\kappa_{*})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\epsilon}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*})\,\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\leq c(\kappa_{*})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\epsilon}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*})\,\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}\,\epsilon^{2}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

In addition, using case (1) of Lemma
4.4
, we may estimate

(229)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

Using that the remainder coefficients in the expansions (
205
), (
226
), (
226
), and (
227
) each satisfy a Lipschitz bound of the form (
213
), and using the bounds (
81
), given two curves
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, the forms of the above kernels also yield

(230)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rVert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rVert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rVert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rVert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rVert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

We now turn to bounds for the derivatives of
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},i}
,
i=1,2,3
, along the surface
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
. We begin with
s
-derivatives and note the expansions

(231)

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{R}

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}(s,\theta))\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}(s,\theta)

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t}

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}(s,\theta))\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)+\widehat{s}(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}(s)+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}}(s))+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}(s,\theta)

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R}

\displaystyle=\textstyle\widehat{s}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs1}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rs2}

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{r}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})=\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}(s,\theta)-\kappa_{3}\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs3}

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}

\displaystyle=\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{r}(s,\theta)=0\,,\qquad\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)=(1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}(s,\theta))\,,\qquad\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta}(s,\theta)=\epsilon\kappa_{3}\,.

Again the remainders
Q_{{\rm Rs}j}
each satisfy estimates of the form (
213
).
We may then write

(232)

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t}

\displaystyle=\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})-\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta})

\displaystyle=\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{S,6}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})\,,

where
Q_{S,5}
and
Q_{S,6}
also satisfy (
213
).

We begin by estimating
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1}
, which we again consider in two pieces as
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}+\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}
. Note that we will treat the commutator term
[\partial_{s},\Phi^{-1}]
as part of the smoother remainder
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}
.
For each
j={\rm t},r,\theta
, we may write

\displaystyle\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}

\displaystyle={\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}S_{1,1}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}+\frac{\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}}{8\pi}\cdot\big[\partial_{s},\Phi^{-1}\big]\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}\bm{\varphi}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,,

\displaystyle S_{1,1}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t}-\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}\bigg)(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t})\bigg)\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{S,6}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}\bigg)(\widehat{s}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}-\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))\bigg)\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,.

Using (
227
) and Lemma
4.2
along with the commutator estimate (
78
), we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*})\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

In addition, for
\alpha<\beta<1
, by case (1) of Lemma
4.4
we have

(233)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\beta}\left(\max_{i}\left\lVert Q_{i}\right\rVert_{C_{1}^{0,\beta}}\right)\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

Here the
Q_{i}
include the remainder coefficients
Q_{S,3}
,
Q_{S,4}
,
Q_{S,5}
, and
Q_{S,6}
.
Furthermore, given two filaments with centerlines
\bm{X}^{(a)}
,
\bm{X}^{(b)}
as in Lemma
1.1
, we have

\displaystyle S_{1,1}^{(a)}-S_{1,1}^{(b)}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}(Q_{S,6}^{(a)}-Q_{S,6}^{(b)})}{|\bm{R}^{(a)}|^{3}}+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}^{(a)}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}^{(b)}|^{3}}\bigg)\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{S,6}^{(b)}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\frac{1}{\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(a)}\big|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}^{(a)}|^{3}}\bigg)(\epsilon\widehat{s}(Q_{S,5}^{(a)}-Q_{S,5}^{(b)})-\epsilon^{2}(\kappa_{3}^{(a)}-\kappa_{3}^{(b)})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\frac{1}{\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(a)}\big|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}^{(a)}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{\big|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(b)}\big|^{3}}+\frac{1}{|\bm{R}^{(b)}|^{3}}\bigg)(\widehat{s}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}^{(b)}-\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}^{(b)}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))\bigg)\bm{e}_{j}^{(a)}\cdot\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{S,6}^{(b)}}{|\bm{R}^{(b)}|^{3}}+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}^{(b)}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}^{(b)}|^{3}}\bigg)(\widehat{s}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}^{(b)}-\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}^{(b)}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))\bigg)\big(\bm{e}_{j}^{(a)}\cdot\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}-\bm{e}_{j}^{(b)}\cdot\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}\big)\,.

Since each remainder coefficient satisfies the Lipschitz estimate (
213
), using (
209
), (
227
), and Lemma
4.4
, case (1), along with the bounds (
81
) and (
82
), we have

(234)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\beta}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

Similarly, for
j={\rm t},r,\theta
, we write

\displaystyle\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}

\displaystyle={\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}S_{1,2}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,,

\displaystyle S_{1,2}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}}-\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}\bigg)\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}}\bigg)\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle=\bigg(-\frac{\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}-\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta})}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}+\widehat{s}\bigg(\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}\bigg)\bigg)\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,.

Using (
227
) and Lemmas
4.3
and
4.2
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1+\alpha}\left(\max_{i}\left\lVert Q_{i}\right\rVert_{C_{2}^{0,\alpha}}\right)\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,,

where the
Q_{i}
include
Q_{S,1}
,
Q_{S,2}
, and
Q_{S,5}
.
In addition, using case (2) of Lemma
4.4
along with (
81
) and (
82
), we have

(235)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon\,\max_{i}\left(\left\lVert Q_{i}\right\rVert_{C_{1}^{0,\alpha^{+}}}+\left\lVert Q_{i}\right\rVert_{C_{2}^{0,\alpha}}\right)\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

We next consider
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2}
, which we again treat in two parts as
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}
and
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}
. Again, we will treat the commutator term
[\partial_{s},\Phi^{-1}]
as part of the smoother remainder
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}
.
For each
j\in\{{\rm t},r,\theta\}
, we expand the components of
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}
as

\displaystyle\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}

\displaystyle={\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\big(S_{2,1,j}+S_{2,2,j}+S_{2,3,j}\big)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}}{8\pi}\cdot\big[\partial_{s},\Phi^{-1}\big]\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\bm{R}(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,,

\displaystyle S_{2,1,j}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\partial_{s}\bm{R})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}

\displaystyle S_{2,2,j}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{R})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}

\displaystyle S_{2,3,j}

\displaystyle=-3\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{R})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\varphi})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}+3\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}\,.

Using the expansions (
202
), (
205
), (
231
), and (
232
), we may further expand the tangential direction integrand as

\displaystyle S_{2,1,\rm t}

\displaystyle=\frac{-\widehat{s}^{2}(1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})(\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}\bigg)\textstyle(1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle S_{2,2,\rm t}

\displaystyle=\frac{-\widehat{s}^{3}Q_{\rm t}((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(1+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}\bigg)\widehat{s}(1+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})\big((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\widehat{s}(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}\big)\cdot\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle S_{2,3,\rm t}

\displaystyle=\frac{3\widehat{s}^{3}Q_{S,6}(1+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\widehat{s}^{3}\big[Q_{\rm t}(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})+(1+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\big]\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs1}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle-3\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle\widehat{s}(1+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs2}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))\,,

where we define
\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}=\bm{e}_{\rm t}-\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}
,
\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs1}=1+\epsilon Q_{\rm Rs1}+\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs2}
, and
\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs2}=1+\epsilon Q_{\rm Rs1}+\widehat{s}(Q_{\rm Rs2}-Q_{S,6})
. Using the expansion (
227
) for
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}
and
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}
, along with the commutator bound (
78
), we may apply Lemma
4.4
, case (1), to obtain, for
\alpha<\beta<1
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{e}_{z}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\beta}\left(\max_{i}\left\lVert Q_{i}\right\rVert_{C_{1}^{0,\beta}}\right)\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

Here
Q_{i}
is used as a catch-all notation for each of the coefficients appearing in the expressions above, including
\widehat{\kappa}(s,\theta)
,
\kappa_{j}(s)
,
Q_{\rm t}
,
\bm{Q}_{\rm t}
,
\bm{Q}_{r}
, and additional
Q
remainders.
In addition, using (
213
), (
210
), (
227
), (
81
), and (
82
), by Lemma
4.4
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{e}_{z}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\bm{e}_{z}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\beta}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

We next expand the
\bm{e}_{r}
-direction integrand of the first integral in
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}
using (
202
), (
205
), (
231
), and (
232
). We have

\displaystyle S_{2,1,r}

\displaystyle=\frac{-\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}\big(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\big)\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}\bigg)\textstyle\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle S_{2,2,r}

\displaystyle=\frac{-\widehat{s}^{2}(\bm{e}_{r}\cdot\bm{Q}_{\rm t})((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{\widehat{s}(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{0,5})(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}\bigg)\textstyle(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{0,5})((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\widehat{s}(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle S_{2,3,r}

\displaystyle=\frac{3\widehat{s}^{3}\widehat{\kappa}Q_{S,6}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\widehat{s}^{2}\big[(\bm{e}_{r}\cdot\bm{Q}_{\rm t})(\widehat{s}\widehat{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})+(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{0,5})\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\big]\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs1}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle-3\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{0,5})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs2}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))\,,

where
\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}
,
\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs1}
, and
\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs2}
are as defined above. We again use the expansion (
227
) for
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}
and
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}
, along with the commutator estimate (
78
), and apply case (1) of Lemma
4.4
to obtain, for
\alpha<\beta<1
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\overline{\bm{e}}_{r}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\epsilon^{1-\beta}\left(\max_{i}\left\lVert Q_{i}\right\rVert_{C_{1}^{0,\beta}}\right)\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta},c_{\Gamma})\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\overline{\bm{e}}_{r}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\overline{\bm{e}}_{r}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\beta}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

Finally, we expand the
\bm{e}_{\theta}
-direction integrand of the first integral of
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}
using (
202
), (
205
), (
231
), and (
232
). We have

\displaystyle S_{2,1,\theta}

\displaystyle=\frac{-\widehat{s}\,\partial_{\theta}\widehat{\kappa}(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}(\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})(\epsilon\kappa_{3}+\widehat{s}\partial_{\theta}\widehat{\kappa})}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}\bigg)\textstyle(\epsilon\kappa_{3}+\widehat{s}\partial_{\theta}\widehat{\kappa})\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\big)\cdot\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle S_{2,2,\theta}

\displaystyle=\frac{-\widehat{s}^{2}(\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{Q}_{\rm t})((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{\widehat{s}(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}\bigg)\textstyle(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\widehat{s}(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle S_{2,3,\theta}

\displaystyle=\frac{3\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{S,6}(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\widehat{s}^{2}\big[\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{Q}_{\rm t}(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})+\epsilon(\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\big]\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs1}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle-3\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle\epsilon(\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs2}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))\,,

with
\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}
,
\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs1}
, and
\widetilde{Q}_{\rm Rs2}
as before. Again expanding
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}
and
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}
using (
227
), and using the commutator estimate (
78
), by Lemma
4.4
, case (1), we have, for
\alpha<\beta<1
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\beta}\left(\max_{i}\left\lVert Q_{i}\right\rVert_{C_{1}^{0,\beta}}\right)\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\beta}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

In total, for
\alpha<\beta<1
, we have

(236)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\beta}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

We now turn to
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}
. For
j\in\{{\rm t},r,\theta\}
, we again expand the components of
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}
as

\displaystyle\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}

\displaystyle={\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\big(S_{2,4,j}+S_{2,5,j}+S_{2,6,j}\big)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,,

\displaystyle S_{2,4,j}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi}))}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}

\displaystyle S_{2,5,j}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}})(\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi}))}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}

\displaystyle S_{2,6,j}

\displaystyle=-3\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm t})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}+3\frac{(\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\varphi}))(\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\,.

Similar to before, we use (
196
), (
202
), and (
232
) to expand the tangential direction integrand as

\displaystyle S_{2,4,\rm t}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}+\epsilon\widehat{s}(\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}\bigg)\textstyle(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle S_{2,5,\rm t}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon\widehat{s}(\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}+\frac{\widehat{s}(1+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\widehat{s}(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}\bigg)\widehat{s}(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})

\displaystyle S_{2,6,\rm t}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\epsilon\widehat{s}(\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\widehat{s}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}-\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}

\displaystyle-\frac{3\epsilon\widehat{s}\big[\widehat{s}(\widehat{s}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}-\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))\bm{Q}_{r}+(\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}-\epsilon\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})\big]\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-3\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle\widehat{s}^{2}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,.

Here most terms appear with an additional factor of
\epsilon
compared to those appearing in
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}
, including the terms involving
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}
and
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}
, by (
227
). The second term in
S_{2,5,\rm t}
does not carry an additional factor of
\epsilon
in all components. In particular, there is a term of the form
\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm t}\right\rvert^{3}}
. We emphasize that this term instead includes an additional factor of
\widehat{s}
in the numerator, and that
\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}}
depends only on
s
and not
\theta
. By case (1) of Lemma
4.4
, this term satisfies

\displaystyle\left\lvert\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm t}\right\rvert^{3}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}_{ss}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}(\mathbb{T})}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

In particular, since
\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}}
depends only on
s
, we do not lose an additional factor of
\epsilon^{-\alpha}
.
The remaining expressions making up
\bm{e}_{z}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}
may be estimated using case (2) of Lemma
4.4
. In total, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{e}_{z}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon\,\max_{i}\left(\left\lVert Q_{i}\right\rVert_{C_{1}^{0,\alpha^{+}}}+\left\lVert Q_{i}\right\rVert_{C_{2}^{0,\alpha}}\right)\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

By similar arguments and estimate (
81
), for two filaments with centerlines
\bm{X}^{(a)}
,
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, we may additionally obtain the Lipschitz estimate

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{e}_{z}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}^{(a)}-\bm{e}_{z}\cdot\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}^{(b)}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

We next consider
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}
in directions normal to the filament centerline. It will be convenient to consider
S_{2,4,{\rm n_{1}}}
and
S_{2,4,{\rm n_{2}}}
(in the directions
\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}
and
\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}}
) instead of
S_{2,4,r}
and
S_{2,4,\theta}
to avoid introducing artificial
\theta
-dependence resulting in a loss of
\epsilon^{-\alpha}
. The expressions
S_{2,4,{\rm n_{1}}}
and
S_{2,4,{\rm n_{2}}}
are given by

\displaystyle S_{2,4,{\rm n_{1}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\widehat{s}\kappa_{1}(s)-\epsilon\kappa_{3}\sin(\theta))(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}

\displaystyle S_{2,4,{\rm n_{2}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\widehat{s}\kappa_{2}(s)+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\cos(\theta))(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}\,.

Both expressions contain terms proportional to
\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}
,
\frac{\widehat{s}\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}
, and terms which are higher order in
\epsilon
. For the terms of the first form, using case (1) of Lemma
4.4
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}\kappa_{i}(s)\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm t}\right\rvert^{3}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}_{ss}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}(\mathbb{T})}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,

since
\kappa_{i}(s)\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)
,
i=1,2
, depends only on
s
and not
\theta
or
\widehat{\theta}
. In particular, we do not lose an additional factor of
\epsilon^{-\alpha}
from estimating these terms in
C^{0,\alpha}
. For terms proportional to
\frac{\widehat{s}\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}
, we treat
\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})
as a coefficient, i.e. let
\bm{Q}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})=2\kappa_{i}(s)\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}(s,\theta)
, and use case (2) of Lemma
4.4
to estimate

(237)

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm t}\right\rvert^{3}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon\,\left(\left\lVert\bm{Q}\right\rVert_{C_{1}^{0,\alpha^{+}}}+\left\lVert\bm{Q}\right\rVert_{C_{2}^{0,\alpha}}\right)\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

The remaining terms may be treated using case (2) of Lemma
4.4
, yielding, for
j\in\{{\rm n_{1},n_{2}}\}
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}S_{2,4,j}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

In addition, using (
213
), (
210
), (
227
), and (
81
), by Lemma
4.4
, case (2), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\big(S_{2,4,j}^{(a)}-S_{2,4,j}^{(b)}\big)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

We next use (
196
), (
202
), and (
232
) to expand the remaining terms in the
\bm{e}_{r}
- and
\bm{e}_{\theta}
-direction integrands as

\displaystyle S_{2,5,r}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{0,5}(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}+\frac{(\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{0,5}+2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))(-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\widehat{s}(\kappa_{1}(s)\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}(s)+\kappa_{2}(s)\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}}(s))+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}\bigg)\textstyle 2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})

\displaystyle S_{2,6,r}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\epsilon\widehat{s}\big[\widehat{s}Q_{0,5}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})+2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{Q}_{r}\big]\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\widehat{s}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}-\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{6\epsilon^{2}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}-\epsilon\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle 6\widehat{s}\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle S_{2,5,\theta}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon\widehat{s}(\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}+\frac{\epsilon(\widehat{s}(\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})+\sin(\widehat{\theta}))(-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\widehat{s}(\kappa_{1}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}}+\kappa_{2}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{3}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{3}}\bigg)\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi})

\displaystyle S_{2,6,\theta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\epsilon\widehat{s}\big[(\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}+\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r})+\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})\bm{Q}_{r}\big]\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\widehat{s}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}-\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}Q_{S,5}-\epsilon\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-3\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm t}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})\widehat{s}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,.

The second and third terms in
S_{2,5,r}
and
S_{2,6,r}
both contain components that must be treated as in (
237
) to avoid loss of
\epsilon^{-\alpha}
. In total, using case (2) of Lemma
4.4
, for both
j=r
and
j=\theta
we obtain the bound

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\big(S_{2,5,j}+S_{2,6,j}\big)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\big(S_{2,5,j}^{(a)}-S_{2,5,j}^{(b)}+S_{2,6,j}^{(a)}-S_{2,6,j}^{(b)}\big)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

Altogether, for
\alpha^{+}
satisfying
\alpha<\alpha^{+}<1
, we have

(238)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

Finally, we derive bounds for
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}
, given by

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=-\Phi^{-1}\,{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}(\partial_{s}\mathcal{G})\,\bm{\varphi}\,\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}-[\partial_{s},\Phi^{-1}]\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\mathcal{G}\,\bm{\varphi}\,\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

We may write the integrand of the first term of
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}
as

\displaystyle\big(\partial_{s}\mathcal{G}\,\bm{\varphi}\big)\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}=\bigg(-\frac{(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R})\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}+\frac{\partial_{s}\bm{R}(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}+\frac{\bm{R}(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\varphi})}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}-3\frac{\bm{R}(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\varphi})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}\bigg)\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}

\displaystyle=\bigg(-\frac{(\widehat{s}(1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\widehat{s}\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}+\frac{(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}}{|\bm{R}|^{3}}

\displaystyle-3\frac{(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta})\cdot\bm{\varphi}\,(\widehat{s}(1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+2\widehat{s}\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}+\epsilon^{2}\kappa_{3}\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}\bigg)\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}\,.

Noting the additional factor of
\epsilon
due to (
8
), by Lemma
4.4
, case (2), we then have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\Phi^{-1}\,{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}(\partial_{s}\mathcal{G})\,\bm{\varphi}\,\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

Using the commutator bound (
78
) to estimate the second term of
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}
, we then have

(239)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}^{(a)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(a)}}]-\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}^{(b)}[\bm{\varphi}_{0}^{\Phi^{(b)}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

We may now put everything together. Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},+}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}[\bm{\varphi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,1}[\bm{\varphi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,1}[\bm{\varphi}]\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},\epsilon}[\bm{\varphi}]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},1,2}[\bm{\varphi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},2,2}[\bm{\varphi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},3}[\bm{\varphi}]\,.

Then, combining the bounds (
224
), (
228
), (
229
), (
230
), (
233
), (
234
), (
236
), we obtain the estimates (
67
) and (
68
) for
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},+}
.
Additionally, combining the bounds (
228
), (
229
), (
230
), (
235
), (
238
), and (
239
), we obtain the estimates (
67
) and (
68
) for
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},\epsilon}
. In total, we obtain Lemma
1.10
.
∎

4.3.
Double layer remainder terms

We next prove Lemma
1.11
regarding the mapping properties of the double layer remainder
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}
, defined in (
65
). We again restate the lemma here for convenience.

Lemma 4.6

(Double layer remainder)
.

Let
0<\gamma<\beta<1
and consider a filament
\Sigma_{\epsilon}
with centerline
\bm{X}(s)\in C^{2,\beta}(\mathbb{T})
. Let the double layer remainder
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}
be as defined in (
65
). Given
\bm{\psi}\in C^{0,\gamma}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})
, we have that
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}
satisfies

(240)

\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rVert_{C^{1,\gamma}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}

for any
\gamma^{+}\in(\gamma,\beta]
.
Furthermore, given two nearby filaments with centerlines
\bm{X}^{(a)}(s)
,
\bm{X}^{(b)}(s)
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, the difference between their corresponding double layer remainders
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(a)}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(b)}
satisfies

(241)

\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(a)}[\bm{\psi}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(b)}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rVert_{C^{1,\gamma}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

Proof of Lemma
1.11
.

Note that although we do not actually need an estimate for the full
C^{1,\gamma}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})
norm of
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}
, as
C_{s}^{1,\gamma}
will suffice, we show the full bound here because the double layer estimates will be used later on in bounding the full Neumann data
\bm{w}
(Lemma
1.13
).

We begin by introducing some notation. Given a vector-valued density
\bm{\psi}\in C^{0,\gamma}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})
and matrix-valued kernel
\bm{H}
defined along
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, we denote

(242)

\displaystyle\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\bm{H})

\displaystyle=\Phi^{-1}\,{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\bm{H}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})\bm{\psi}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}\,d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle-{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\overline{\bm{H}}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}\,d\widehat{s}\,,

where
\overline{\bm{H}}
is the corresponding matrix kernel defined along the straight filament
\partial\mathcal{C}_{\epsilon}
. Noting that
\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\bm{H})
lives on straight basis vectors, we use
W_{j}
to denote the scalar-valued kernel resulting from dotting
\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\bm{H})
with the straight basis vector
\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}
,
j\in\{{\rm t},r,\theta\}
:

(243)

\displaystyle\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\bm{H})\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}

\displaystyle={\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}W_{j}(\bm{\psi};\bm{H})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,;

\displaystyle W_{j}(\bm{\psi};\bm{H})

\displaystyle:=(\bm{H}\bm{\psi})\cdot\bm{e}_{j}(s)-\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot(\overline{\bm{H}}\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})\,.

We again note that
\overline{\bm{e}}_{\rm t}=\bm{e}_{z}
, but here we will use the overline notation for convenience.

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}
from (
65
) and rewriting the integrands in terms of
(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})
, we may write

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}[\bm{\psi}(s,\theta)]

\displaystyle=\Phi^{-1}\mathcal{D}[\bm{\psi}(s,\theta)]-\overline{\mathcal{D}}[(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})(s,\theta)]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},0}[\bm{\psi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}[\bm{\psi}]+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}[\bm{\psi}]\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},0}[\bm{\psi}]

\displaystyle=-\bigg(\int_{-\infty}^{-1/2}+\int_{1/2}^{\infty}\bigg)\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\overline{\bm{K}}_{\mathcal{D}}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}[\bm{\psi}]

\displaystyle=\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}[\bm{\psi}]

\displaystyle=-\Phi^{-1}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})\bm{\psi}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

Here the form of
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}
is as defined in (
242
) and the terms
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},0}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}
arise due to the integration limits in the straight setting and the Jacobian factor (
8
), respectively.

As we did for the single layer, we begin with bounds for the smooth remainder
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},0}
away from the singularity at
(\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})=(0,0)
. Using the form (
38
) of the straight kernel
\overline{\bm{K}}_{\mathcal{D}}
and the expression (
196
) for
\overline{\bm{R}}
, we have

(244)

\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},0}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rVert_{C^{2}}\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{1/2}^{\infty}\bigg(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\widehat{s}\right\rvert^{2}}+\frac{1}{\left\lvert\widehat{s}\right\rvert^{3}}+\frac{1}{\left\lvert\widehat{s}\right\rvert^{4}}\bigg)\,d\widehat{s}\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

In addition, given two nearby curves
\bm{X}^{(a)}
,
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, the corresponding remainders
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},0}^{(a)}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},0}^{(b)}
are nearly identical except for the map
\Phi
identifying the tangential and normal components of
\bm{\psi}
with straight basis vectors. In particular, we may estimate

(245)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},0}^{(a)}[\bm{\psi}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{S},0}^{(b)}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rVert_{C^{2}}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\bigg(\int_{-\infty}^{-1/2}+\int_{1/2}^{\infty}\bigg)\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\overline{\bm{K}}_{\mathcal{D}}\,((\Phi^{(a)})^{-1}\bm{\psi}-(\Phi^{(b)})^{-1}\bm{\psi})\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rVert_{C^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c\,\epsilon\left\lVert(\Phi^{(a)})^{-1}\bm{\psi}-(\Phi^{(b)})^{-1}\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*}^{(a)},\kappa_{*}^{(b)})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,

by (
79
).

For the remainders
\mathcal{R}_{{\mathcal{D}},1}
and
\mathcal{R}_{{\mathcal{D}},2}
, we start with
L^{\infty}
bounds. Using the form (
38
) of
\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}
, we may write the integrands
W_{j}
given by (
243
) as

\displaystyle W_{j}(\bm{\psi};\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})=\frac{3}{4\pi}\bigg(\frac{\bm{e}_{j}(s)\cdot\bm{R}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}\,(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})\big(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})\big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\bm{e}_{j}(s)\cdot\bm{R}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}\big(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime}-\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime}\big)\,\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})+\frac{(\bm{e}_{j}(s)\cdot\bm{R})-(\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})\,\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)(\overline{\bm{e}}_{j}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})\,\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})\bigg)\,.

Using the expansions (
211
) and (
212
) along with (
210
) and Lemma
4.1
, for each
j=t,r,\theta
, we may bound

\displaystyle\left\lvert W_{j}(\bm{\psi};\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|}\,.

Using the above integrands in the definition (
243
) of
\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})=\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}
, by Lemma
4.2
we have

(246)

\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

In addition, for two filaments with centerlines
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, we may use the above expansion of
W_{j}(\bm{\psi};\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})
along with (
210
), (
211
), (
212
), and the bounds (
213
) for each remainder term
Q
appearing in the expansion to obtain

(247)

\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}^{(a)}[\bm{\psi}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}^{(b)}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*}^{(a)},\kappa_{*}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,

by Lemma
4.2
.
Furthermore, using the form (
38
) of
\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}
and (
211
), we have

(248)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\epsilon^{-1}}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|}\,\epsilon^{2}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\leq\epsilon\,c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}^{(a)}[\bm{\psi}]-\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}^{(b)}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*}^{(a)},\kappa_{*}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

We now turn to
\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}
estimates for the components
\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{{\mathcal{D}},1}
,
\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{{\mathcal{D}},2}
,
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{{\mathcal{D}},1}
,
\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{{\mathcal{D}},2}
of the gradient of
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}}
along the surface
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
.
We note the following expansions:

(249)
