\displaystyle\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}

\displaystyle=\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}_{\rm t}=\bm{e}_{\theta}(s,\theta)

\displaystyle\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R}

\displaystyle=\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2}

\displaystyle\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{r}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})=-\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 3}

\displaystyle\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}

\displaystyle=\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{r}(s,\theta)=0\,,\qquad\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)=0\,,\qquad\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta}(s,\theta)=1\,.

Here the terms
Q_{{\rm R\theta}j}
each satisfy bounds of the form (
213
).

We start with the
\theta
-derivatives
\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}
and
\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}
, which may be written as follows:

\displaystyle\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}[\bm{\psi}]

\displaystyle=\bm{W}[\bm{\psi};\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}]

\displaystyle\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}[\bm{\psi}]

\displaystyle=-\Phi^{-1}\,{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}(s,\widehat{s},\theta,\widehat{\theta})\bm{\psi}(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

We begin by estimating
\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}[\bm{\psi}]
. Using the definition (
243
) of the integrands
W_{j}
, we may write
W_{\rm t}(\bm{\psi};\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})
as

\displaystyle W_{\rm t}(\bm{\psi};

\displaystyle\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})=\displaystyle\frac{3}{4\pi}\big(W_{\rm t,1}+W_{\rm t,2}+W_{\rm t,3}\big)\,,

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,1}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\bm{e}_{z})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)(\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\bm{e}_{z})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,3}

\displaystyle=-\frac{5(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}+\frac{5(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\bm{e}_{z})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\,.

Using the expansions (
211
), (
212
), and (
249
), we may write the components
W_{{\rm t},j}
in more detail:

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,1}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{(\epsilon\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rt})(-2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})+\widehat{s}^{3}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle 2\epsilon\widehat{s}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bigg)(\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\epsilon\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rt})(-\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 3})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 3}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}\textstyle(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\widehat{s}\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot{\bm{\psi}}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle\widehat{s}\sin(\widehat{\theta})\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{10\epsilon\widehat{s}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\varphi})(\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{5\widehat{s}^{3}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\varphi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\varphi})(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{5\widehat{s}(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})(-2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm n^{\prime}})(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle+\frac{5(\epsilon\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rt})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm n^{\prime}})(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\quad-\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\bigg)\textstyle 10\epsilon^{2}\widehat{s}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})\,,

where
\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}=\bm{e}_{\rm t}-\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}
. Using the expansion (
209
), (
210
) for
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}
and
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}
, we may apply Lemma
4.4
to obtain

(250)

\displaystyle\textstyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})\cdot\bm{e}_{z}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\max_{j}\big(\left\lVert Q_{j}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma^{+}}_{1}}+\left\lVert Q_{j}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}_{2}}\big)\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

Here the
\max_{j}
is taken over all forms of remainder terms appearing in the above expansions. We also note that terms of the form, for example,
\frac{\widehat{s}^{4}\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}
may be treated as
\frac{\widehat{s}^{4}\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\widetilde{Q}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}
,
\widetilde{Q}=\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q
, so as not to lose a factor of
\epsilon^{-\gamma}
from using case (1) of Lemma
4.4
.

Furthermore, using the above expansions of
W_{{\rm t},j}
and the Lipschitz estimates (
213
) for each remainder
Q_{j}
, we additionally note that, given two curves
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, we have

(251)

\displaystyle\textstyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}(\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(a)}-\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(b)}))\cdot\bm{e}_{z}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

We next consider the integrand
W_{r}(\bm{\psi};\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})
as defined in (
243
). We may write

\displaystyle\textstyle W_{r}(\bm{\psi};\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{4\pi}\big(W_{r,1}+W_{r,2}+W_{r,3}\big)\,,

\displaystyle W_{r,1}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{r})\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{r})\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime}}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)(\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})

\displaystyle W_{r,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{r})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{r})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{r,3}

\displaystyle=-\frac{5(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{r})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}+\frac{5(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{r})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\,.

Again by (
211
), (
212
), and (
249
), we may further expand each
W_{r,j}
as

\displaystyle W_{r,1}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{-2\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(Q_{\rm Rn}+Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})+\widehat{s}^{4}Q_{\rm Rn}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\textstyle 4\epsilon^{2}\sin^{4}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\bigg)(\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})

\displaystyle W_{r,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn}(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(-\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 3})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\big[(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\sin(\widehat{\theta})-\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 3})+\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 3}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})\big]}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle 2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})

\displaystyle W_{r,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{5\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn}(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{10\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{10\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{20\epsilon^{2}\sin^{4}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\bigg)\textstyle 20\epsilon^{3}\sin^{4}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})\,.

Here again
\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}=\bm{e}_{\rm t}-\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}
, and we may use the expansion (
209
), (
210
) for
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}
and
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}
. We may then apply Lemma
4.4
to obtain

(252)

\displaystyle\textstyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{r}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\max_{j}\big(\left\lVert Q_{j}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma^{+}}_{1}}+\left\lVert Q_{j}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}_{2}}\big)\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,,

\displaystyle\textstyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}(\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(a)}-\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(b)}))\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{r}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

Where necessary, we again may exchange some regularity for a factor of
\epsilon^{\gamma}
by treating terms of the form, e.g.,
\frac{\widehat{s}^{4}\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}
as
\frac{\widehat{s}^{4}\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\widetilde{Q}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}
with
\widetilde{Q}=\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q
. Then case (2) of Lemma
4.4
yields the bound (
252
).

Finally we turn to the integrand
W_{\theta}(\bm{\psi};\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})
as defined in (
243
). We may write

\displaystyle W_{\theta}(\bm{\psi};

\displaystyle\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})=\displaystyle\frac{3}{4\pi}\big(W_{\theta,1}+W_{\theta,2}+W_{\theta,3}+W_{\theta,4}\big)\,,

\displaystyle W_{\theta,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{\theta,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{\theta,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{\theta,4}

\displaystyle=-\frac{5(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}+\frac{5(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{R}})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\,.

We may again use (
211
), (

), and (
249
) to expand each

as

\displaystyle W_{\theta,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle 2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})

\displaystyle W_{\theta,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\epsilon\widehat{s}\kappa_{3}\cos(\theta-\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta})(-2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})+\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}(\bm{\psi}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})(-2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\bm{\psi}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})

\displaystyle W_{\theta,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\epsilon\widehat{s}\kappa_{3}\cos(\theta-\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(-\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 3})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})\widehat{s}\big[(\sin(\widehat{\theta})-\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 3})(\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})+Q_{\rm R\theta 3}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})\big]}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle\epsilon\sin^{2}(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})

\displaystyle W_{\theta,4}

\displaystyle=\frac{5(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\epsilon\widehat{s}\kappa_{3}\cos(\theta-\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{5\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})(-2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{5\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{10\epsilon^{2}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta 1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm R\theta 2})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\bigg)\textstyle 10\epsilon^{3}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin^{2}(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})\,.

As before, using the expansions (

), (

(253)

\displaystyle\textstyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\max_{j}\big(\left\lVert Q_{j}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma^{+}}_{1}}+\left\lVert Q_{j}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}_{2}}\big)\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,,

\displaystyle\textstyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}(\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(a)}-\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(b)}))\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

Combining (

\displaystyle\textstyle\left\lvert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\textstyle\left\lvert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}^{(a)}[\bm{\psi}]-\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},1}^{(b)}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

We next turn to

, which we note may be written

\displaystyle\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}[\bm{\psi}]

\displaystyle=-\bm{W}[\bm{\psi};\widehat{\kappa}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}]-{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\textstyle\widehat{\kappa}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{K}}_{\mathcal{D}}\,(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})(s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

Note the additional factor of

is due to the Jacobian factor (

Using that (

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{W}[\bm{\psi};\widehat{\kappa}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{W}[\bm{\psi};\widehat{\kappa}^{(a)}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(a)}-\widehat{\kappa}^{(b)}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(b)}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,,

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{10\epsilon^{2}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})\widehat{s}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot{\bm{\psi}})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\textstyle\overline{\bm{e}}_{r}\cdot\big(\widehat{\kappa}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{K}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{3\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}}{4\pi}\bigg(-\frac{2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}+\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt-\frac{\epsilon\sin^{2}(\widehat{\theta})\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}+\frac{10\epsilon^{3}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin^{2}(\widehat{\theta})\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\textstyle\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta}\cdot\big(\widehat{\kappa}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{K}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{3\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}}{4\pi}\bigg(-\frac{4\epsilon^{2}\sin^{4}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}-\frac{2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{20\epsilon^{3}\sin^{4}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\bigg)\,.

Using Lemma
4.4
, we thus obtain

(255)

\displaystyle\textstyle\left\lvert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lvert\bm{W}[\bm{\psi};\widehat{\kappa}\partial_{\theta}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}+\bigg|\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\textstyle\widehat{\kappa}\partial_{\theta}\overline{\bm{K}}_{\mathcal{D}}\,(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg|_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,,

\displaystyle\textstyle\left\lvert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}^{(a)}[\bm{\psi}]-\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}^{(b)}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

We now turn to

estimates for the

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}[\bm{\psi}]

\displaystyle=-\Phi^{-1}{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}\,\bm{\psi}\,\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}-[\partial_{s},\Phi^{-1}]\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}\,\bm{\psi}\,\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

We may estimate
\bm{W}[\bm{\psi};\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}]
by first using the definition (

) of the integrands

to write

\displaystyle W_{\rm t}(\bm{\psi};

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})=\frac{3}{4\pi}\big(W_{\rm t,1}+W_{\rm t,2}+W_{\rm t,3}\big)\,,

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})+(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})\big)\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{\big(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})+(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\bm{e}_{z})(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi})\big)\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime}}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\bm{e}_{z})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,3}

\displaystyle=-\frac{5(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})\bm{R}\cdot\partial_{s}\bm{R}}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}+\frac{5(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\bm{e}_{z})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\,.

Using the expansions (
211
), (

), and (

), we may expand each

as follows:

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi}+\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})\big)(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\big((\epsilon\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rt})((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot{\bm{\psi}}+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})-\epsilon\widehat{s}(\widehat{\kappa}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot{\bm{\psi}}-\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})\big)(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle 4\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)

\displaystyle W_{\rm t,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\epsilon\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rt})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}-\kappa_{3}\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs3})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{\widehat{s}(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}-\kappa_{3}\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs3})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle=\frac{5(\epsilon\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rt})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm R}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{5\widehat{s}(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm R}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{5\widehat{s}^{3}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm R}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{10\widehat{s}\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs1}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rs2})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

As above,
\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}=\bm{e}_{\rm t}-\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{r}

\widetilde{Q}_{\rm R}=1+\epsilon Q_{\rm Rs1}+\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs2}

\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}

209
) and (
210
), we may use Lemma
4.4
to estimate

(256)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})\cdot\bm{e}_{z}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\max_{j}\big(\left\lVert Q_{j}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma^{+}}_{1}}+\left\lVert Q_{j}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}_{2}}\big)\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

Furthermore, using that each remainder term
Q_{i}
satisfies a Lipschitz estimate of the form (
213
), by the above expansions and Lemma
4.4
we have

(257)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(a)}-\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(b)})\cdot\bm{e}_{z}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

We next consider
W_{r}(\bm{\psi};\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})
as given by (
243
). We write

\displaystyle W_{r}(\bm{\psi};

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})=\frac{3}{4\pi}\big(W_{r,1}+W_{r,2}+W_{r,3}\big)\,,

\displaystyle W_{r,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{r})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{r})(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi})\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime}}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{r,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{r})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{r})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{r,3}

\displaystyle=-\frac{5(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{r})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})\bm{R}\cdot\partial_{s}\bm{R}}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}+\frac{5(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{r})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\,.

Using (
211
), (
212
), and (
231
), we may expand each
W_{r,j}
as

\displaystyle W_{r,1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn}((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot{\bm{\psi}}+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot{\bm{\psi}}-\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle 4\epsilon^{2}\sin^{4}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi})

\displaystyle W_{r,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn}(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}-\kappa_{3}\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs3})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}-\kappa_{3}\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs3})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\textstyle 2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}-\kappa_{3}\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs3})

\displaystyle W_{r,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{5\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn}(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm R}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{10\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm R}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{10\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm R}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{20\epsilon^{2}\sin^{4}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs1}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rs2})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\bigg)\textstyle 20\epsilon^{2}\sin^{4}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))\,.

Again we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{r}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(a)}-\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(b)})\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{r}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

We finally turn to

\displaystyle W_{\theta,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\epsilon\kappa_{3}(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})+(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})\big)\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime}}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta})(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi})\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime}}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{\theta,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}-\frac{(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{\theta,3}

\displaystyle=-\frac{5(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{e}_{\theta})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})\bm{R}\cdot\partial_{s}\bm{R}}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}+\frac{5(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta})(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi}))(\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\overline{\bm{n}}^{\prime})\overline{\bm{R}}\cdot\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{R}}}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\,.

By (

), (

), and (

\displaystyle W_{\theta,1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{(\epsilon\widehat{s}\kappa_{3}\cos(\theta-\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta})((1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa})\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot{\bm{\psi}}+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{\epsilon\kappa_{3}(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot{\bm{\psi}}+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle W_{\theta,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\epsilon\widehat{s}\kappa_{3}\cos(\theta-\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}\widehat{\kappa}-\kappa_{3}\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})+\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs3})}{|\bm{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle=\frac{5(\epsilon\widehat{s}\kappa_{3}\cos(\theta-\widehat{\theta})+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm R\theta})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{\bm{Q}}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm R}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{5\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}^{2}\bm{Q}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}-\epsilon\widehat{s}\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{\psi})(2\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})-\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm R}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{5\epsilon\sin(\widehat{\theta})\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rn^{\prime}}(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}\widetilde{Q}_{\rm R}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{10\epsilon^{2}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\epsilon\widehat{s}Q_{\rm Rs1}+\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{\rm Rs2})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}\bigg)\textstyle 10\epsilon^{2}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi})(\widehat{s}+2\epsilon^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta}))\,.

We again expand
\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{7}}-\frac{1}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}})\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{W}(\bm{\psi};\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(a)}-\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}^{(b)})\cdot\overline{\bm{e}}_{\theta}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

We finally turn to

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{4\epsilon\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\textstyle\overline{\bm{e}}_{r}\cdot\big(\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{K}}_{\mathcal{D}}(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{3\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}}{4\pi}\bigg(\frac{20\epsilon^{2}\sin^{4}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\widehat{s}\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}-\frac{4\epsilon^{2}\sin^{4}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{3\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}}{4\pi}\bigg(\frac{10\epsilon^{2}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})\widehat{s}\big(\widehat{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi}+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}\cdot\bm{\psi}\big)}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{7}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{2\epsilon^{2}\sin^{2}(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})\sin(\widehat{\theta})(\bm{e}_{\rm t}\cdot\bm{\psi})}{|\overline{\bm{R}}|^{5}}\bigg)\,.

By Lemma
4.4
, we thus have

(263)

\displaystyle\textstyle\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}\leq\left\lvert\bm{W}[\bm{\psi};\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}\partial_{s}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}+\displaystyle\bigg|{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\textstyle\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}\partial_{s}\overline{\bm{K}}_{\mathcal{D}}\,(\Phi^{-1}\bm{\psi})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg|_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle+\left\lvert[\partial_{s},\Phi^{-1}]\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}\,\bm{\psi}\,\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\kappa}\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,,

\displaystyle\textstyle\displaystyle\left\lvert\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}^{(a)}[\bm{\psi}]-\partial_{s}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D},2}^{(b)}[\bm{\psi}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\gamma}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-\gamma^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{\psi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,.

Combining the estimates (
244
), (
245
), (
246
), (
247
), (
248
), (
254
), (
255
), (
262
), and (
263
), we obtain Lemma
1.11
.
∎

4.4.
Single layer applied to constant-in-arclength

This section is devoted to the proof of Lemma
1.12
regarding the angle-averaged single layer operator applied to functions that are constant in
s
. We restate the lemma here for convenience.

Lemma 4.7

(Single layer applied to constant-in-
s
)
.

Let
0<\alpha<\beta<1
and consider a filament
\Sigma_{\epsilon}
with centerline
\bm{X}(s)\in C^{2,\beta}(\mathbb{T})
. Given
\overline{\bm{h}}(\theta)\in C^{0,\alpha}(2\pi\mathbb{T})
of the form
\overline{\bm{h}}=h_{1}(\theta)\bm{e}_{z}+h_{2}(\theta)\bm{e}_{x}+h_{3}(\theta)\bm{e}_{y}
, and recalling the map
\Phi
(
27
), we may decompose

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}(\theta)]=\mathcal{H}_{\epsilon}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}(\theta)]+\mathcal{H}_{+}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}(\theta)]

where

(264)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{H}_{\epsilon}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}]\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,\quad\left\lvert\mathcal{H}_{\epsilon}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1,\alpha}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{H}_{+}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}]\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,,\quad\left\lvert\mathcal{H}_{+}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1,\beta}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

for any
\alpha^{+}\in(\alpha,\beta]
.
In addition, given two nearby curves
\bm{X}^{(a)}
,
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, the corresponding differences
\mathcal{H}_{\epsilon}^{(a)}-\mathcal{H}_{\epsilon}^{(b)}
and
\mathcal{H}_{+}^{(a)}-\mathcal{H}_{+}^{(b)}
satisfy

(265)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{H}_{\epsilon}^{(a)}[\Phi^{(a)}\overline{\bm{h}}]-\mathcal{H}_{\epsilon}^{(b)}[\Phi^{(b)}\overline{\bm{h}}]\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{H}_{\epsilon}^{(a)}[\Phi^{(a)}\overline{\bm{h}}]-\mathcal{H}_{\epsilon}^{(b)}[\Phi^{(b)}\overline{\bm{h}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha^{+}}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{H}_{+}^{(a)}[\Phi^{(a)}\overline{\bm{h}}]-\mathcal{H}_{+}^{(b)}[\Phi^{(b)}\overline{\bm{h}}]\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathcal{H}_{+}^{(a)}[\Phi^{(a)}\overline{\bm{h}}]-\mathcal{H}_{+}^{(b)}[\Phi^{(b)}\overline{\bm{h}}]\right\rvert_{\dot{C}_{s}^{1,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\beta}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

Proof of Lemma
1.12
.

For the proof of this lemma, it will be convenient to keep our integrands in terms of
s^{\prime}
and
\theta^{\prime}
instead of
\widehat{s}=s-s^{\prime}
and
\widehat{\theta}=\theta-\theta^{\prime}
. The expansions and bounds of section
4.1
will thus be applied using
s-s^{\prime}
and
\theta-\theta^{\prime}
in place of
\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta}
.

We will first define an auxiliary function akin to the function
\bm{R}_{\rm t}
appearing in the proof of Lemma
1.10
. Let

(266)

\displaystyle\bm{R}_{\rm r}

\displaystyle=\bm{X}(s)-\bm{X}(s^{\prime})+\epsilon\big(\bm{e}_{r}(s,\theta)-\bm{e}_{r}(s,\theta^{\prime})\big)

\displaystyle=\textstyle\bm{X}(s)-\bm{X}(s^{\prime})+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta-\theta^{\prime}}{2})\bm{e}_{\theta}(s,\frac{\theta+\theta^{\prime}}{2})\,,

and note that we have

(267)

\displaystyle\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r}

\displaystyle=\epsilon\big(\bm{e}_{r}(s,\theta^{\prime})-\bm{e}_{r}(s^{\prime},\theta^{\prime})\big)=\epsilon(s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{r}

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{2}-\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle=2\epsilon(s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm r}+\epsilon^{2}(s-s^{\prime})^{2}\left\lvert\bm{Q}_{r}\right\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{k}}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{k}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\epsilon(s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm r}+\epsilon^{2}(s-s^{\prime})^{2}\left\lvert\bm{Q}_{r}\right\rvert^{2}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert|\bm{R}_{\rm r}|(\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert+|\bm{R}_{\rm r}|)}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k-1}\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{\ell}|\bm{R}_{\rm r}|^{k-1-\ell}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm r}^{(a)}|^{k}}-\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm r}^{(b)}|^{k}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\widehat{s}^{2}\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})A_{ab}+\widehat{s}^{4}B_{ab}}{|\bm{R}_{\rm r}^{(a)}||\bm{R}_{\rm r}^{(b)}|(|\bm{R}_{\rm r}^{(a)}|+|\bm{R}_{\rm r}^{(b)}|)}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k-1}\frac{1}{|\bm{R}_{\rm r}^{(a)}|^{\ell}|\bm{R}_{\rm r}^{(b)}|^{k-1-\ell}}

where
A_{ab}=\bm{e}_{\theta}^{(a)}\cdot\bm{Q}_{\rm t}^{(a)}-\bm{e}_{\theta}^{(b)}\cdot\bm{Q}_{\rm t}^{(b)}
and
B_{ab}=2Q_{\rm t}^{(a)}-2Q_{\rm t}^{(b)}+|\bm{Q}_{\rm t}^{(b)}|^{2}-|\bm{Q}_{\rm t}^{(a)}|^{2}
. In addition,
\bm{R}_{\rm r}
may be shown to satisfy each of Lemmas
4.1
,
4.2
,
4.3
, and
4.4
.

Using
\bm{R}_{\rm r}
, we will then write
\mathcal{S}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}(\theta)]
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}(\theta)]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(\bm{H}_{1}+\bm{H}_{2}+\bm{H}_{3}\big)\,(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})(s^{\prime},\theta^{\prime})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\,,

\displaystyle\bm{H}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{{\bf I}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert}+\frac{\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{3}}

\displaystyle\bm{H}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bm{R}\otimes\bm{R}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}-\frac{\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{3}}=\frac{(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})\otimes\bm{R}+\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}+\bigg(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{3}}\bigg)\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}

\displaystyle\bm{H}_{3}

\displaystyle=-\bigg(\frac{{\bf I}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}+\frac{\bm{R}\otimes\bm{R}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}\bigg)\,\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}(s^{\prime},\theta^{\prime})\,.

Using (
267
) along with Lemmas
4.2
and
4.4
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\bm{H}_{1}\,(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\bm{H}_{2}\,(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\bm{H}_{3}\,(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

In addition, using (
267
) along with the fact that each remainder
\bm{Q}
satisfies (
213
), we may show that for two curves
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(\bm{H}_{1}^{(a)}\,(\Phi^{(a)}\overline{\bm{h}})-\bm{H}_{1}^{(b)}\,(\Phi^{(b)}\overline{\bm{h}})\big)\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(\bm{H}_{2}^{(a)}\,(\Phi^{(a)}\overline{\bm{h}})-\bm{H}_{2}^{(b)}\,(\Phi^{(b)}\overline{\bm{h}})\big)\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(\bm{H}_{3}^{(a)}\,(\Phi^{(a)}\overline{\bm{h}})-\bm{H}_{3}^{(b)}\,(\Phi^{(b)}\overline{\bm{h}})\big)\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{2-\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

We next turn to estimates for
\partial_{s}\mathcal{S}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}(\theta)]
. We first note that, since
\overline{\bm{h}}(\theta)
is independent of
s
, the only
s
-dependence in
(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})(s,\theta)
is due to the map
\Phi
, given by (
27
). We may calculate

\displaystyle\partial_{s}(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})(s,\theta)

\displaystyle=(\partial_{s}\bm{e}_{\rm t})(\overline{\bm{h}}\cdot\bm{e}_{z})+(\partial_{s}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{1}})(\overline{\bm{h}}\cdot\bm{e}_{x})+(\partial_{s}\bm{e}_{\rm n_{2}})(\overline{\bm{h}}\cdot\bm{e}_{y})\,.

In particular, we may bound

(268)

\left\lVert\partial_{s}(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})}\leq c(\kappa_{*})\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(2\pi\mathbb{T})}\,,\quad\left\lVert\partial_{s}(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha})\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(2\pi\mathbb{T})}\,.

In addition, we note the following expansion for
\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}
:

(269)

\displaystyle-\partial_{s^{\prime}}\bm{R}_{\rm r}

\displaystyle=\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s^{\prime})

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}

\displaystyle=\textstyle\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)+2\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta-\theta^{\prime}}{2})\big(\partial_{\theta}\widehat{\kappa}(s,\theta)\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)-\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{r}(s,\theta)\big)

\displaystyle=:\textstyle-\partial_{s^{\prime}}\bm{R}_{\rm r}+(s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{\rm t}(s,s^{\prime})+\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta-\theta^{\prime}}{2})\bm{Q}_{X1}\,.

The closeness of
\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}
to
-\partial_{s^{\prime}}\bm{R}_{\rm r}
will be of particular importance in estimating the kernel
\bm{H}_{1}
. Finally, we record the following expansions of
\partial_{s}\bm{R}
and
\partial_{s}(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})
:

(270)

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{R}

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}(s,\theta))\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}(s,\theta)=:\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)+\epsilon\bm{Q}_{X2}(s,\theta)

\displaystyle\partial_{s}(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})

\displaystyle=\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}(s,\theta^{\prime})\bm{e}_{\rm t}(s)+\epsilon\kappa_{3}\bm{e}_{\theta}(s,\theta^{\prime})=:\epsilon\bm{Q}_{X2}(s,\theta^{\prime})\,.

Here, as usual, the remainders
\bm{Q}_{X1}
and
\bm{Q}_{X2}
satisfy (
213
).

We may use the expansion (
269
) of
\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}
to write
\bm{H}_{1}
as

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{{\bf I}(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm r})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{3}}+\frac{\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}+\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{3}}-\frac{3\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}\,(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm r})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{5}}

\displaystyle=-\partial_{s^{\prime}}\bm{H}_{1}+\frac{\big((s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta-\theta^{\prime}}{2})\bm{Q}_{X1}\big)\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}+\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\big((s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta-\theta^{\prime}}{2})\bm{Q}_{X1}\big)}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{{\bf I}\big((s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta-\theta^{\prime}}{2})\bm{Q}_{X1}\big)\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{3}}-\frac{3\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}\,\big((s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{\rm t}+\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta-\theta^{\prime}}{2})\bm{Q}_{X1}\big)\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{5}}\,.

For the kernel
-\partial_{s^{\prime}}\bm{H}_{1}
, we may integrate by parts in
s^{\prime}
and use Lemma
4.4
, case (1), along with (
268
) to obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\partial_{s^{\prime}}\bm{H}_{1}\,(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}

\displaystyle=\left\lvert\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\bm{H}_{1}\,\partial_{s^{\prime}}(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

For the remaining terms in the above expansion of
\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{1}
, we may use Lemma
4.4
, case (1), to estimate

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{1}\,(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

Using the expansion for
\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{1}
and that each remainder
\bm{Q}
satisfies (
213
), we may use a similar series of arguments to show that, for two curves
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{1}^{(a)}\,(\Phi^{(a)}\overline{\bm{h}})-\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{1}^{(b)}\,(\Phi^{(b)}\overline{\bm{h}})\big)\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\beta}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq c(\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\beta}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-2\beta}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\beta}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\,.

We next consider the kernel
\bm{H}_{2}
. Using (
267
) and (
270
), we may write

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{2}=\frac{\partial_{s}\bm{R}\otimes(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}+\frac{\bm{R}\otimes\partial_{s}(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}-\frac{3\bm{R}\otimes(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\partial_{s}(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}+\frac{(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})\otimes\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}-\frac{3(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-3\frac{\partial_{s}(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})\cdot\bm{R}+\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\cdot(\bm{R}-\bm{R}_{\rm r})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{5}}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{3}}\bigg)\big(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}+\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\big)-3\bigg(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{5}}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm r}\right\rvert^{5}}\bigg)(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm r})\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon(s-s^{\prime})\partial_{s}\bm{R}\otimes\bm{Q}_{r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}+\frac{\epsilon\bm{R}\otimes\bm{Q}_{X2}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}-\frac{3\epsilon(s-s^{\prime})\bm{R}\otimes\bm{Q}_{r}(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\epsilon\bm{Q}_{X2}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}+\frac{\epsilon(s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{r}\otimes\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}-\frac{3\epsilon(s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}(\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{5}}

\displaystyle\qquad-3\frac{\epsilon\bm{Q}_{X2}\cdot\bm{R}+\epsilon(s-s^{\prime})\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\cdot\bm{Q}_{r}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{5}}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}

\displaystyle\quad-\bigg(\frac{2\epsilon(s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm r}+\epsilon^{2}(s-s^{\prime})^{2}\left\lvert\bm{Q}_{r}\right\rvert^{2}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert|\bm{R}_{\rm r}|(\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert+|\bm{R}_{\rm r}|)}\sum_{\ell=0}^{2}\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{\ell}|\bm{R}_{\rm r}|^{2-\ell}}\bigg)\big(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}+\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+3\bigg(\frac{2\epsilon(s-s^{\prime})\bm{Q}_{r}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm r}+\epsilon^{2}(s-s^{\prime})^{2}\left\lvert\bm{Q}_{r}\right\rvert^{2}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert|\bm{R}_{\rm r}|(\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert+|\bm{R}_{\rm r}|)}\sum_{\ell=0}^{4}\frac{1}{|\bm{R}|^{\ell}|\bm{R}_{\rm r}|^{4-\ell}}\bigg)(\partial_{s}\bm{R}_{\rm r}\cdot\bm{R}_{\rm r})\bm{R}_{\rm r}\otimes\bm{R}_{\rm r}\,.

Note that each term contains an additional factor of
\epsilon
. Using Lemma
4.4
, case (2), we thus have

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{2}\,(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

In addition, using that each of the remainder terms
\bm{Q}
satisfy (
213
), we may use the above expansion of
\bm{H}_{2}
to show that, for two curves
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{2}^{(a)}\,(\Phi^{(a)}\overline{\bm{h}})-\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{2}^{(b)}\,(\Phi^{(b)}\overline{\bm{h}})\big)\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha^{+}}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

Finally, we turn to the kernel
\bm{H}_{3}
. We have

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{3}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{{\bf I}\,\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}+\frac{\partial_{s}\bm{R}\otimes\bm{R}+\bm{R}\otimes\partial_{s}\bm{R}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}+3\frac{(\bm{R}\otimes\bm{R})\,\partial_{s}\bm{R}\cdot\bm{R}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{5}}\bigg)\epsilon\widehat{\kappa}(s^{\prime},\theta^{\prime})\,.

Noting the additional factor of
\epsilon
and using the form (
270
) of
\partial_{s}\bm{R}
, we may use Lemma
4.4
to obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{3}\,(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

Furthermore, for two nearby curves
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert{\rm p.v.}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{3}^{(a)}\,(\Phi^{(a)}\overline{\bm{h}})-\partial_{s}\bm{H}_{3}^{(b)}\,(\Phi^{(b)}\overline{\bm{h}})\big)\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\right\rvert_{\dot{C}^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq c(\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\alpha^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{1-\alpha^{+}}\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\alpha^{+}}}\left\lVert\overline{\bm{h}}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\,.

Defining

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{+}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}]=\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\bm{H}_{1}\,(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})(s^{\prime},\theta^{\prime})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\,,\quad\mathcal{H}_{\epsilon}[\Phi\overline{\bm{h}}]=\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{\mathbb{T}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(\bm{H}_{2}+\bm{H}_{3}\big)\,(\Phi\overline{\bm{h}})(s^{\prime},\theta^{\prime})\,\epsilon\,d\theta^{\prime}ds^{\prime}\,,

we obtain Lemma
1.12
.
∎

5.
Bounds for full Neumann data

This section is concerned with the bounds for the full Neumann data
\bm{w}(s,\theta)=-\bm{\sigma}[\bm{u}]\bm{n}\big|_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}
stated in Lemma
1.13
. We restate the lemma below for convenience.

Lemma 5.1

(Bounds for
\bm{w}(s,\theta)
, restated)
.

Let
0<\alpha<\gamma<\beta<1
and consider a slender body
\Sigma_{\epsilon}
with centerline
\bm{X}(s)\in C^{2,\beta}(\mathbb{T})
.
Given
\theta
-independent Dirichlet data
\bm{v}(s)\in C^{1,\alpha}(\mathbb{T})
along the filament surface
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, let
\bm{u}(\bm{x})
,
\bm{x}\in\Omega_{\epsilon}
, denote the corresponding solution to the Stokes equations in
\Omega_{\epsilon}
, and let
\bm{w}(s,\theta)
denote the corresponding Neumann boundary value
\bm{w}=-\bm{\sigma}[\bm{u}]\bm{n}\big|_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}
. Then
\bm{w}
may be decomposed as

\displaystyle\bm{w}(s,\theta)=\bm{w}_{\epsilon}(s,\theta)+\bm{w}_{+}(s,\theta)

where

(271)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{w}_{\epsilon}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\left\lVert\bm{v}\right\rVert_{C^{1,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{w}_{+}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\epsilon,\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\left\lVert\bm{v}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}

for any
\gamma^{+}\in(\gamma,\beta]
.
In addition, given two nearby filaments
\Sigma_{\epsilon}^{(a)},\Sigma_{\epsilon}^{(b)}
with centerlines
\bm{X}^{(a)}
and
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, we have

(272)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{w}_{\epsilon}^{(a)}-\bm{w}_{\epsilon}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*}^{(a)},\kappa_{*}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2}}\left\lVert\bm{v}\right\rVert_{C^{1,\alpha}}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{w}_{+}^{(a)}-\bm{w}_{+}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\epsilon,\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(a)},\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}}^{(b)},c_{\Gamma})\,\left\lVert\bm{X}^{(a)}-\bm{X}^{(b)}\right\rVert_{C^{2,\gamma^{+}}}\left\lVert\bm{v}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}}\,,

where
\bm{w}^{(a)},\bm{w}^{(b)}
denote the Neumann data along filaments
\Sigma_{\epsilon}^{(a)}
and
\Sigma_{\epsilon}^{(b)}
, respectively.

In both (
73
) and (
74
), the
\epsilon
-dependence is explicit in the bounds for
\bm{w}_{\epsilon}
but not for
\bm{w}_{+}
.

The proof of Lemma
1.13
will again rely on a boundary integral formulation of the full Stokes Dirichlet-to-Neumann map along the slender body surface
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
. However, it will be more convenient to use a different formulation from the one presented in section
1.2.4
in order to extract more precise information.

Following
[
62
,
61
]
, given Dirichlet data
\bm{v}(\bm{x})
on
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, the Stokes flow outside of the slender body
\Sigma_{\epsilon}
may be represented by

(273)

\bm{u}(\bm{x})=\mathcal{D}[\bm{\varphi}](\bm{x})+\bm{V}[\bm{\varphi}](\bm{x})

where
\mathcal{D}
is the double layer operator given by (
39
) and
\bm{V}
is a
completion flow
serving the following purpose. Along the slender body surface, the density
\bm{\varphi}
must satisfy the integral equation

(274)

\bm{v}(\bm{x})=\textstyle(\frac{1}{2}{\bf I}+\mathcal{D})[\bm{\varphi}](\bm{x})+\bm{V}[\bm{\varphi}](\bm{x})\,,\qquad\bm{x}\in\Gamma_{\epsilon}\,,

where the
\frac{1}{2}{\bf I}
arises from the jump condition for the double layer operator on
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
. Since the operator
\frac{1}{2}{\bf I}+\mathcal{D}
has a well-known 6-dimensional nullspace along
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
consisting of rigid motions
[
62
]
, an appropriate choice of completion flow
\bm{V}
is necessary for invertibility.

There are many possible choices for
\bm{V}
(see
[
16
,
61
]
). We will use a form of
\bm{V}
proposed by
[
35
]
that is particularly well-suited for slender filaments.
Consider
\bm{x}^{\prime}\in\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, denoted by
\bm{x}^{\prime}=\bm{X}(s^{\prime})+\epsilon\bm{e}_{r}(s^{\prime},\theta^{\prime})
, and consider the velocity field
\bm{V}
given by

(275)

\bm{V}[\bm{\varphi}](\bm{x})=\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\mathcal{G}(\bm{x},\bm{X}(s^{\prime}))\,\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})\,dS_{x^{\prime}}+\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{l}_{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{x},\bm{X}(s^{\prime}))\times\big((\bm{x}^{\prime}-\bm{X}(s^{\prime}))\times\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})\big)\,dS_{x^{\prime}}\,.

We emphasize that
\bm{X}(s^{\prime})
corresponds to
\bm{x}^{\prime}
while
\bm{x}
is independent.
Here
\bm{l}_{\mathcal{R}}
is a
rotlet
, given by

(276)

\bm{l}_{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{x},\bm{y})=-\frac{(\bm{x}-\bm{y})}{\left\lvert\bm{x}-\bm{y}\right\rvert^{3}}\,,

and describes the Stokes flow due to a point torque at
\bm{y}

[
15
]
. The completion flow (
275
) may be understood as the Stokes flow due to a line density of point forces and point torques distributed along the filament centerline. Note that
\bm{V}
is a smooth function of
\bm{x}
along
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
since these singularities lie along the centerline of the filament rather than the surface.

Given the representation (
273
), (
274
) of the Stokes flow
\bm{u}(\bm{x})
in
\Omega_{\epsilon}
with Dirichlet data
\bm{v}(\bm{x})
, we may calculate the corresponding Neumann boundary value for
\bm{u}
along
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
. We begin by noting the following expression for the stress due to the double layer
\mathcal{D}[\bm{\varphi}](\bm{x})
,
\bm{x}\in\Omega_{\epsilon}
:

(277)

\displaystyle\bm{\sigma}[\mathcal{D}[\bm{\varphi}]](\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\nabla_{x}\mathcal{D}[\bm{\varphi}]+(\nabla_{x}\mathcal{D}[\bm{\varphi}])^{\rm T}-p^{\mathcal{D}}[\bm{\varphi}](\bm{x}){\bf I}\,,

\displaystyle p^{\mathcal{D}}[\bm{\varphi}](\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{p}^{\mathcal{D}}(\bm{x},\bm{x}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})\,dS_{x^{\prime}}\,,

\displaystyle\bm{p}^{\mathcal{D}}(\bm{x},\bm{x}^{\prime};\bm{n}(\bm{x}^{\prime}))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\bigg(-\frac{\bm{n}(\bm{x}^{\prime})}{\left\lvert\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime}\right\rvert^{3}}+3\frac{(\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime})(\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{n}(\bm{x}^{\prime})}{\left\lvert\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime}\right\rvert^{5}}\bigg)\,.

Here
p^{\mathcal{D}}
is the pressure associated with the double layer representation of
\bm{u}
and
\bm{p}^{\mathcal{D}}
is the vector-valued pressure kernel for this representation
[
15
,
47
,
62
]
.

Letting
\bm{R}=\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime}
,
\bm{n}=\bm{n}(\bm{x})
, and
\bm{n}^{\prime}=\bm{n}(\bm{x}^{\prime})
, we define the following kernel corresponding to the double layer stress tensor (
277
):

(278)

\displaystyle\bm{K}_{\mathcal{T}}(\bm{x},\bm{x}^{\prime};\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\pi}\bigg(2\frac{\bm{n}\otimes\bm{n}^{\prime}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{3}}+3\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})\bm{R}\otimes\bm{n}+(\bm{n}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})\bm{R}\otimes\bm{R}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{5}}

\displaystyle+3\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime}){\bf I}+(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n})\bm{n}^{\prime}\otimes\bm{R}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{5}}-30\frac{(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n})(\bm{R}\cdot\bm{n}^{\prime})\bm{R}\otimes\bm{R}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{7}}\bigg)\,.

Then for
\bm{x}\in\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, on the surface of the filament, we may consider the stress associated to the double layer operator as

(279)

\bm{\sigma}[\mathcal{D}[\bm{\varphi}]]\bm{n}(\bm{x})={\rm p.v.}\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{T}}(\bm{x},\bm{x}^{\prime};\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime})\big(\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})-\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x})\big)\,dS_{x^{\prime}}\,.

Here the normal vectors
\bm{n}
and
\bm{n}^{\prime}
both point out of the slender body
\Sigma_{\epsilon}
, into the fluid domain
\Omega_{\epsilon}
.

To obtain the representation (
279
), we use that both the double layer kernel
\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}
(
38
) and the corresponding pressure kernel
\bm{p}^{\mathcal{D}}
(
277
) integrate to zero within the exterior domain
\Omega_{\epsilon}
, i.e.

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}(\bm{y},\bm{x}^{\prime})\,dS_{x^{\prime}}=0\,,\quad\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{p}^{\mathcal{D}}(\bm{y},\bm{x}^{\prime};\bm{n}(\bm{x}^{\prime}))\,dS_{x^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=0\,,\qquad\bm{y}\in\Omega_{\epsilon}\,.

For any constant-in-
\bm{y}
vector-valued surface density
\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x})
,
\bm{x}\in\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, we then have

\displaystyle\nabla_{y}\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}(\bm{y},\bm{x}^{\prime})\,\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x})\,dS_{x^{\prime}}=0\,,\quad\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{p}^{\mathcal{D}}(\bm{y},\bm{x}^{\prime};\bm{n}(\bm{x}^{\prime}))\cdot\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x})\,dS_{x^{\prime}}=0\,;\qquad\bm{y}\in\Omega_{\epsilon}\,,\;\bm{x}\in\Gamma_{\epsilon}\,.

Putting these together, for
\bm{x}\in\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, we may understand
\bm{\sigma}[\mathcal{D}[\bm{\varphi}]]\bm{n}(\bm{x})
as

\displaystyle\bm{\sigma}[\mathcal{D}[\bm{\varphi}]]\bm{n}(\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\lim_{\bm{y}\to\bm{x}}\bigg(\nabla_{y}\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}(\bm{y},\bm{x}^{\prime})\,\big(\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})-\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x})\big)\,dS_{x^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\nabla_{y}\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{D}}(\bm{y},\bm{x}^{\prime})\,\big(\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})-\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x})\big)\,dS_{x^{\prime}}\bigg)^{\rm T}

\displaystyle\qquad-{\bf I}\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{p}^{\mathcal{D}}(\bm{y},\bm{x}^{\prime};\bm{n}(\bm{x}^{\prime}))\cdot(\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})-\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}))\,dS_{x^{\prime}}\bigg)\bm{n}(\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\lim_{\bm{y}\to\bm{x}}\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bm{K}_{\mathcal{T}}(\bm{y},\bm{x}^{\prime};\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime})\big(\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})-\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x})\big)\,dS_{x^{\prime}}\,,\quad\bm{y}\in\Omega_{\epsilon}\,.

We may also calculate the stress corresponding to the completion flow
\bm{V}
, given by

\displaystyle\bm{\sigma}[\bm{V}[\bm{\varphi}]](\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\nabla_{x}\bm{V}[\bm{\varphi}]+(\nabla_{x}\bm{V}[\bm{\varphi}])^{\rm T}+p^{V}(\bm{x}){\bf I}\,,

\displaystyle p^{V}(\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\pi}\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\frac{(\bm{x}-\bm{X}(s^{\prime}))\cdot\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})}{\left\lvert\bm{x}-\bm{X}(s^{\prime})\right\rvert^{3}}\,dS_{x^{\prime}}\,.

Here
p^{V}
is the pressure corresponding to the flow
\bm{V}

[
47
]
. For
\bm{x}\in\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, we define
\bm{R}_{X}=\bm{x}-\bm{X}(s^{\prime})
and let
\bm{n}=\bm{n}(\bm{x})
. Along
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, we may then write

(280)

\displaystyle\bm{\sigma}[\bm{V}[\bm{\varphi}]]\bm{n}(\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{8\pi}\int_{\Gamma_{\epsilon}}\bigg(-2\frac{\big(\bm{R}_{X}\cdot\bm{n}\big)(\bm{R}_{X}\cdot\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime}))\bm{R}_{X}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{X}\right\rvert^{5}}+\frac{\bm{R}_{X}\cdot\bm{n}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{X}\right\rvert^{5}}\,\bm{R}_{X}\times\big((\bm{x}^{\prime}-\bm{X}(s^{\prime}))\times\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{\bm{R}_{X}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{X}\right\rvert^{5}}\,\bm{n}\cdot\big(\bm{R}_{X}\times\big((\bm{x}^{\prime}-\bm{X}(s^{\prime}))\times\bm{\varphi}(\bm{x}^{\prime})\big)\big)\bigg)\,dS_{x^{\prime}}\,.

Given the representations (
279
) and (
280
), we define the hypersingular operator
\mathcal{T}
to be the Neumann boundary value associated with the flow (
273
), given by

(281)

\mathcal{T}[\bm{\varphi}](\bm{x})=-\bm{\sigma}[\mathcal{D}[\bm{\varphi}]]\bm{n}(\bm{x})-\bm{\sigma}[\bm{V}[\bm{\varphi}]]\bm{n}(\bm{x})\,,\qquad\bm{x}\in\Gamma_{\epsilon}\,.

Here we note that the negative sign arises since
\bm{n}
points into the fluid domain, out of the slender body
\Sigma_{\epsilon}
.

Given Dirichlet data
\bm{v}(\bm{x})
along
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
, we may use the completed double layer representation (
273
), (
274
) of
\bm{u}
to calculate the corresponding Neumann boundary value
\bm{w}
via

(282)

\bm{w}(\bm{x})=\textstyle\mathcal{T}\big[(\frac{1}{2}{\bf I}+\mathcal{D}+\bm{V})^{-1}[\bm{v}]\big](\bm{x})\,,\qquad\bm{x}\in\Gamma_{\epsilon}\,.

We will use the representation (
282
) of the full Neumann boundary value
\bm{w}
to prove Lemma
1.13
. In order to do so, we will rely on the following series of lemmas bounding each of the components of (
282
). We state each lemma below and then immediately use the lemmas to prove Lemma
1.13
. The proof of each component lemma is then given in subsequent sections
5.1
,
5.2
, and
5.3
.

We begin with bounds for the completed double layer operator (
274
) along
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
.

Lemma 5.2

(Completed double layer smoothing)
.

Let
0<\gamma<\beta<1
and consider a filament
\Sigma_{\epsilon}
with centerline
\bm{X}\in C^{2,\beta}
. Given
\bm{\varphi}\in C^{1,\alpha}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})
, the double layer operator
\mathcal{D}
(
39
) and completion flow
\bm{V}
(
275
) along
\Gamma_{\epsilon}
satisfy

(283)

\left\lVert(\mathcal{D}+\bm{V})[\bm{\varphi}]\right\rVert_{C^{1,\gamma}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})}\leq c(\kappa_{*,\gamma^{+}},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{-1-\gamma}\left\lVert\bm{\varphi}\right\rVert_{C^{0,\gamma}(\Gamma_{\epsilon})}

for any
\gamma^{+}\in(\gamma,\beta]
.
Furthermore, given two nearby filaments with centerlines
\bm{X}^{(a)}
,
\bm{X}^{(b)}
satisfying Lemma
1.1
, we have

(284)
