\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}I_{\varphi,\ell kn}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,\bigg|\,.

We begin by considering the case
\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}\geq\epsilon
. Using the first expansion (
371
) for
\varphi
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\widehat{I}_{\varphi}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\widehat{s}^{\ell}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\,g}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}\bigg(\widehat{s}\partial_{s}\varphi(s,\theta)+\epsilon\widehat{\theta}\,\textstyle\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi(s,\theta)\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\left\lVert\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\widehat{s}^{\ell}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\,g}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}\big(\left\lvert\widehat{s}\right\rvert^{1+\alpha}Q_{\varphi,s}+|\epsilon\widehat{\theta}|^{1+\alpha}Q_{\varphi,\theta}\big)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\epsilon^{\alpha}\bigg(\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}\big(\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\textstyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big)+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big(\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}+\textstyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\,\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}\bigg(\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}\big(\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\textstyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big(\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}+\textstyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\big)\bigg)\,.

Here we have used Lemmas
4.3
and
4.2
and the fact that
\epsilon\leq\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}
.

We next consider the case
\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}<\epsilon
. Throughout, we will use the notation

\displaystyle\bm{R}

\displaystyle=\bm{R}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\,,\qquad\bm{R}_{0}=\bm{R}(s_{0}+s,\theta_{0}+\theta,s_{0}+\widehat{s},\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta})\,,

\displaystyle g_{0}

\displaystyle=g(s_{0}+s,\theta_{0}+\theta,s-\widehat{s},\theta-\widehat{\theta})\,,\qquad g_{0}^{0}=g_{0}\big|_{(s_{0}+\widehat{s},\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta})=(0,0)}\,,\qquad g^{0}=g\big|_{(\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})=(0,0)}

to condense the exposition.
Using the above notation as well as the expansions (
368
) and (
370
), we may write

\displaystyle\widehat{I}_{\varphi}

\displaystyle=J_{\varphi,s}+J_{\varphi,\theta}\,,

\displaystyle J_{\varphi,s}

\displaystyle={\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2-s_{0}}^{1/2-s_{0}}\int_{-\pi-\theta_{0}}^{\pi-\theta_{0}}\frac{(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{\ell}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\,g_{0}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}(s_{0}+\widehat{s})H_{\varphi,s}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\widehat{s}^{\ell}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\,g}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}\big(-\widehat{s}\partial_{s}\varphi(s,\theta)+\left\lvert\widehat{s}\right\rvert^{1+\alpha}Q_{\varphi,s}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\big)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle J_{\varphi,\theta}

\displaystyle={\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2-s_{0}}^{1/2-s_{0}}\int_{-\pi-\theta_{0}}^{\pi-\theta_{0}}\frac{(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{\ell}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\,g_{0}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}\epsilon(\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta})H_{\varphi,\theta}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\qquad-{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\widehat{s}^{\ell}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\,g}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}\bigg(\textstyle-\epsilon\widehat{\theta}\,\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi(s,\theta)+|\epsilon\widehat{\theta}|^{1+\alpha}Q_{\varphi,\theta}(s,\theta,\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

In order to bound these terms, we will need to introduce a function
\bm{R}_{\rm even}
satisfying

(373)

\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert^{2}:=\left\lvert\overline{\bm{R}}\right\rvert^{2}+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{2}Q_{R,0}(s,\theta)+\kappa_{3}\epsilon^{2}\widehat{s}\sin(\widehat{\theta})\,,

where
Q_{R,0}
is as in (
207
), and crucially does not depend on
\widehat{s}
or
\widehat{\theta}
. In particular, since
|\overline{\bm{R}}|
is even about both
\widehat{s}=0
and
\widehat{\theta}=0
, this means that
\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert
is also even about
\widehat{s}=0
and
\widehat{\theta}=0
. For nonnegative integers
n
and
m
, we thus have that the expression

(374)

\frac{\widehat{s}^{n}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{m}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert^{n+m+2}}\,,\quad n+m\text{ odd},

is globally odd about
\widehat{s}=\widehat{\theta}=0
. Therefore we have that

(375)

{\rm p.v.}\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\widehat{s}^{n}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{m}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert^{n+m+2}}\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}=0\,,

and thus kernels of the form (
374
) with constant-in-
\widehat{s}
-and-
\widehat{\theta}
coefficients may be inserted into the integral expressions for
J_{\varphi,s}
and
J_{\varphi,\theta}
.
We additionally note that

(376)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle=\textstyle\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert^{2}+\widehat{s}^{4}Q_{R,1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{3}Q_{R,2}+\epsilon^{2}\widehat{s}^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q_{R,3}\,,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{s}^{4}Q_{R,1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{3}Q_{R,2}+\epsilon^{2}\widehat{s}^{2}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q_{R,3}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert(\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert+\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert)}\,,

where each
Q_{R,j}
is as in (
207
) and, as in Lemma
4.1
,

(377)

\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert\geq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})|\overline{\bm{R}}|\,.

We will use the notation
\bm{R}_{\rm even,0}
to denote
\bm{R}_{\rm even}(s_{0}+s,\theta_{0}+\theta,s_{0}+\widehat{s},\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta})
.

Now, in order to bound
J_{\varphi,s}
and
J_{\varphi,\theta}
, we split the integrals into two regions:

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle=\big\{(\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\;:\;\sqrt{(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{2}+\epsilon^{2}(\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta})^{2}}\leq 4\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}\big\}\,,

\displaystyle R_{2}

\displaystyle=\big\{(\widehat{s},\widehat{\theta})\;:\;\sqrt{(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{2}+\epsilon^{2}(\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta})^{2}}>4\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}\big\}\,.

For
j=s,\theta
, let
J_{\varphi,j,1}
denote the integral of the integrand of
J_{\varphi,j}
over the region
R_{1}
. Note that
\sqrt{\widehat{s}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\theta}^{2}}
then belongs to the region
R_{1}^{\prime}
where
\sqrt{\widehat{s}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\theta}^{2}}\leq 5\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}
.

After inserting
|\bm{R}_{\rm even}|
and
|\bm{R}_{\rm even,0}|
and using the first expansion (
371
) for
\varphi
about
(s_{0}+s,\theta_{0}+\theta)
, we may bound

\displaystyle\left\lvert J_{\varphi,s,1}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lvert\partial_{s}\varphi(s_{0}+s,\theta_{0}+\theta)\,{\rm p.v.}\iint_{R_{1}}\textstyle(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{\ell+1}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{g_{0}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}-\frac{g_{0}^{0}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even,0}\right\rvert^{n}}\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\lvert\partial_{s}\varphi(s,\theta)\,{\rm p.v.}\iint_{R_{1}^{\prime}}\textstyle\widehat{s}^{\ell+1}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{g}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}-\frac{g^{0}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert^{n}}\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\quad+\iint_{R_{1}}\frac{|s_{0}+\widehat{s}|^{\ell+1+\alpha}|\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2})|^{k}\,|g_{0}|}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}\left\lvert Q_{\varphi,s}\right\rvert\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}+\iint_{R_{1}^{\prime}}\frac{|\widehat{s}|^{\ell+1+\alpha}|\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})|^{k}\,|g|}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}\left\lvert Q_{\varphi,s}\right\rvert\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}\iint_{R_{1}}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{2-\alpha}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}+\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}\iint_{R_{1}^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{2-\alpha}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lvert\bigg({\rm p.v.}\iint_{R_{1}}(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{\ell+1}\textstyle(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even,0}\right\rvert^{n}}\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg)g_{0}^{0}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\quad+\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lvert\bigg({\rm p.v.}\iint_{R_{1}^{\prime}}\widehat{s}^{\ell+1}\textstyle(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert^{n}}\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg)g^{0}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\bigg(\iint_{R_{1}}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{2-\alpha}}\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}+\iint_{R_{1}^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{2-\alpha}}\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq c\big(\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}+\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big)\bigg(\iint_{R_{1}}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{2-\alpha}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}+\iint_{R_{1}^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{2-\alpha}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\big(\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}+\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big)\bigg(\iint_{\rho\leq 4\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}}\frac{1}{\rho^{2-\alpha}}\,\rho d\rho d\phi

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt+\iint_{\rho\leq 5\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}}\frac{1}{\rho^{2-\alpha}}\,\rho d\rho d\phi\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\big(\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}+\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big)\,\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}\,.

Here in the second-to-last line we have used that
\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert\geq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\sqrt{\widehat{s}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\widehat{\theta}^{2}}
(see
[
59
, Lemma 3.1]
) to convert to polar coordinates
(\rho,\phi)
over the patch
\rho\leq c\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}<c\epsilon
.
Similarly, we may show that
J_{\varphi,\theta,1}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left\lvert J_{\varphi,\theta,1}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\big(\textstyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}+\left\lVert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big)\,\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}\,.

Finally, we bound
J_{\varphi,s,2}
and
J_{\varphi,\theta,2}
over the region
R_{2}
. We now use the second expansion (
372
) of
\varphi
about
(s_{0}+s,\theta_{0}+\theta)
to write
J_{\varphi,s,2}
as

\displaystyle J_{\varphi,s,2}

\displaystyle=-\partial_{s}\varphi(s,\theta)\iint_{R_{2}}\bigg(\frac{(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{\ell+1}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\,(g_{0}-g_{0}^{0})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}-\frac{\widehat{s}^{\ell+1}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\,(g-g^{0})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\qquad-\partial_{s}\varphi(s,\theta)\iint_{R_{2}}\frac{(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{\ell+1}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\,(g_{0}^{0}-g^{0})}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\qquad-\partial_{s}\varphi(s,\theta)\,g^{0}\iint_{R_{2}}\bigg(\textstyle(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{\ell+1}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even,0}\right\rvert^{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\textstyle\widehat{s}^{\ell+1}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert^{n}}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}\bigg)\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\lvert s_{0}\right\rvert^{1+\alpha}Q_{\varphi,s}(s,\theta,s_{0}+s,\theta_{0}+\theta)\iint_{R_{2}}\frac{(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{\ell}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}\,g_{0}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\,.

Using (
376
) to expand the differences
\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even,0}\right\rvert^{n}}
and
\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert^{n}}-\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert J_{\varphi,s,2}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}\bigg|\iint_{R_{2}}\bigg(\frac{(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{\ell+1+\alpha}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}-\frac{\widehat{s}^{\ell+1+\alpha}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{n}}\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+c\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha^{+}}_{1}}\iint_{R_{2}}\frac{\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha^{+}}\,|s_{0}+\widehat{s}|^{\ell+1}|\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2})|^{k}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{n}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\bigg|\iint_{R_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\widehat{s}^{5}Q_{R,1}+\epsilon\widehat{s}^{4}Q_{R,2}+\epsilon^{2}\widehat{s}^{3}\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q_{R,3}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert(\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert+\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert)}\sum_{j=0}^{n-1}\frac{\widehat{s}^{\ell+1}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}\right\rvert^{j}\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even}\right\rvert^{n-1-j}}

\displaystyle-\frac{(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{5}Q_{R,1}+\epsilon(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{4}Q_{R,2}+\epsilon^{2}(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{3}\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2})Q_{R,3}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even,0}\right\rvert\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert(\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert+\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even,0}\right\rvert)}\sum_{j=0}^{n-1}\frac{(s_{0}+\widehat{s})^{\ell+1}(\epsilon\sin(\frac{\theta_{0}+\widehat{\theta}}{2}))^{k}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{j}\left\lvert\bm{R}_{\rm even,0}\right\rvert^{n-1-j}}\bigg)\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+c\left\lvert s_{0}\right\rvert^{1+\alpha}\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\iint_{R_{2}}\frac{1}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{3}}\,\epsilon d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*})\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\bigg(\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}\iint_{R_{2}}\frac{\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{3-\alpha}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha^{+}}_{1}}\iint_{R_{2}}\frac{\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha^{+}}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{2}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+c(\kappa_{*})\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\bigg(\iint_{R_{2}}\frac{\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}}{\left\lvert\overline{\bm{R}}_{0}\right\rvert^{2}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}+\iint_{R_{2}}\frac{\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}}{\left\lvert\overline{\bm{R}}_{0}\right\rvert}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+c\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\iint_{R_{2}}\frac{\left\lvert s_{0}\right\rvert^{1+\alpha}}{\left\lvert\bm{R}_{0}\right\rvert^{3}}\,\epsilon\,d\widehat{\theta}d\widehat{s}

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*})\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big(\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha^{+}}_{1}}\big)\iint_{\rho>4\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}}{\rho^{3-\alpha}}+\frac{\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha^{+}}}{\rho^{2}}\bigg)\,\rho d\rho d\phi

\displaystyle\quad+c(\kappa_{*})\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\iint_{\rho>4\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}}\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}}{\rho^{2}}+\frac{\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}}{\rho}+\frac{\left\lvert s_{0}\right\rvert^{1+\alpha}}{\rho^{3}}\bigg)\,\rho d\rho d\phi

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*})\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big(\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha^{+}}_{1}}\big)\bigg(\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}\big(1+\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,-1+\alpha}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha^{+}}\big(1+\left\lvert\log(s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2})\right\rvert\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+c(\kappa_{*})\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\bigg(\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}\big(1+\left\lvert\log(s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2})\right\rvert\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}\big(1+\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}\big)+\left\lvert s_{0}\right\rvert^{1+\alpha}\big(1+\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,-1}\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*})\bigg(\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big(\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha^{+}}_{1}}\big)+\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\bigg)\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}\,.

Here in the second inequality we have used the closeness of the kernels with respect to
s_{0}
and
\epsilon\theta_{0}
, and in the third inequality we have again switched to polar coordinates. In the fourth inequality we use that
\alpha^{+}>\alpha
to absorb the logarithmic term in
\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha^{+}}\left\lvert\log(s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2})\right\rvert
.

By an analogous series of estimates, it may be shown that
J_{\varphi,\theta,2}
satisfies

\displaystyle c(\kappa_{*})\bigg(\textstyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big(\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha^{+}}_{1}}\big)+\left\lVert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\bigg)\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}\,.

Combining the estimates from cases 1, 2a, and 2b, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\widehat{I}_{\varphi}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq c(\kappa_{*},c_{\Gamma})\bigg(\big(\textstyle\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\left\lVert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big)\big(\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}_{2}}+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha^{+}}_{1}}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\textstyle\big(\left\lVert\partial_{s}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}+\left\lVert\frac{1}{\epsilon}\partial_{\theta}\varphi\right\rVert_{C^{0,\alpha}}\big)\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\bigg)\sqrt{s_{0}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}^{2}}^{\,\alpha}\,.

∎
